## Ideas and issues
- (P4-07) Pet Care (P4-12) still draws its stage with the old `stageHTML` (48-shop.js): emoji scene decorations (`.sdeco`) and the raw-colour gradient in `SHOP.scene[i][3]`. The shop now uses `.ps-stage` + `hmScene` (Home's drawn scenes); Pet Care can do the same, then `stageHTML` and the emoji / gradient fields of `SHOP.scene` (index 2 and 3) can go (the scene ids stay, they are saved).
- (P4-07) index.html is 1,199,832 bytes, 168 under the 1.2 MB warning. The wearable drawings went into the data file (`src/data/d-wear.js`) for that reason; the next chunk that adds code to index.html will cross the warning (fail is 1.5 MB). Moving `ART` (16-art-a.js) to `src/data` is the obvious next lever (see the A11 note).
- (P4-07) Sticker book stickers are still emoji (collectible content, `STK`), shown die-cut. When object art covers a sticker, `book()` can show `picArt` in `.sb-e` instead; the die-cut shadow would then need an SVG-friendly outline (a white offset copy, not a filter).
- (P4-07) The emoji particle helpers `burst()` / `confetti()` (12-speech.js) are no longer used by the shop or the book (drawn `RW.burst` instead); Pet Care, Home's pet tap, balloons and boss hits still use them (P4-17).
- (P3-16) bt.<VER>.js is 393,523 bytes after the three spatial types (warn at 400,000, fail at 800,000), so the next puzzle chunk will print the part-file warning. Nothing is wrong; if it should stay quiet, comments are about a third of 75- to 86-bt-*.js.
- (P3-16) The catalog name "Cube Count" is shown as "Cubes": with the timer chip (bands C and D) the top bar cut it to "Cube Cou" at 380 px (the `.bt-tt` limit of the P3-05 and P3-10 notes).
- (P3-16) Mirror grids of 8 x 8 are 44 px a square on a 380 px phone (BRAIN_TEASERS 8 allows 44 inside dense grids); the puzzle uses the stage's side padding to get there. Smoke checks 44 for `.mr-cell`, 48 for everything else.
- (P3-16) Cube Count's hint 2 on a one-layer stack (band B levels 1-4) shows a single layer, which adds little; hint 3 then says that layer's count, which is the answer.
- (P3-07) index.html is close to the 1.5 MB fail budget: 1,489,949 bytes with the logic grid UI (80-bt-logic.js 41 KB, 49-bt-logic.css 8 KB). A11b (puzzle-type code in its own precached file) is what makes room; until it lands, the next puzzle UI chunk will not fit in index.html.
- (P3-07) The play top bar shows the type name in one line; "Logic Grid" was cut off at 380 px with the timer chip (bands C and D), so the type is named "Logic" (see the P3-10 note below on `.bt-tt`). **Fixed** in P3-17 (names wrap; "Logic Grid" again).
- (P3-07) Band D on a phone shows one block per tab (Colour | Race | Colour + race): 4x4 with two categories needs 33 px squares to show all three blocks in 348 px. A link (copy marks through a tick) then spans two tabs; the hint words say both marks, and the tab with a clash gets an amber dot, but a child sees one block at a time. On 1024x600 the children's two blocks sit side by side and the third block is on a second tab (the whole grid would need 41 px squares there).
- (P3-07) The bank's band B has only 3 clue shapes (P3-06 note), so band B levels feel alike; the name swap and the 36 pictures/categories vary the look but not the reasoning.

- (P3-11) The type is called "Tangram" (singular, as in BRAIN_TEASERS section 4): "Tangrams" was cut to "Tangram…" in the play top bar at 380x780 when the timer shows (the same `.bt-tt` limit as MathDoku below).
- (P3-11) Tray pieces are drawn smaller than on the board when many wait in the tray on a phone (band D at 380x780: about 18-25 px a unit in the tray, 38-57 on the board); they grow as the tray empties, never past the board's size, and a piece picked up takes the board's size at once. If children find the jump confusing, a later chunk could animate it.
- (P3-11) Tangram mistakes are not counted (a piece in the wrong place is part of trying), so its stars depend on hints only. The "fail or quit the same level 3 times" rule is still the framework's open item (P3-01).
- (P3-13) The play top bar cuts "Robot Path" to "Robot Pat" at 380 px in bands C and D (the timer chip takes the room), like "MathDok…" (P3-05) and "Number P" (P3-10). The catalog name was kept; the framework fix noted there (a smaller title under 400 px, or the name in its own ellipsis span) would fix every type.
- (P3-13) Band D on a phone: with the function strip and a long program the board is about 180 px at 380x780 (22 px squares on 8x8; the board is only looked at, never tapped), while 600x960 and 1024x600 give 330-430 px boards. A fold-away palette or a one-row scrolling program strip would give phones more board.
- (P3-13) "Repeat until wall" is very strong in corridors: the search finds 6-8 block programs that follow almost any corridor by spinning (for example `repeat 7 [forward, turn left, repeat until wall [forward], turn right]`). That is why band D offers it on levels 1-9 and the function on levels 10-12, never both, and why the bank keeps only worlds where the template's natural program is a shortest one. If D should mix both blocks, the worlds need open cells past the corners (so "until wall" overshoots), which the generator does not make yet.
- (P3-13) Par in the stars: the frame rates stars from hints and mistakes only, so a winning program longer than par reports allow + 1 mistakes (2 stars at most). If the frame gets a real "par" input (mazes also show a par without using it), this can move there.

- (P3-06) Band B logic grids (3 children x 1 category, 2-3 clues, as the spec asks) come in only 3 shapes once the clue set is minimal: two "is" clues, one "is" and one "is not", or three "is not". The bank varies the category, items and names (no two puzzles share shape and items), and every B puzzle grades 2 (the last child always needs "only place left"), so B levels differ only by clue count (scores 204-209). If B should ramp more, allow 4 children in its later levels (a spec change).
- (P3-06) The grader's grade is a lower bound in the spirit of human.js: band C always needs the link step (all 36 grade 3) and band D needs the two-way clue step (24 grade 4) or one chain (12 grade 5, levels 9-12). P3-07's hint tier 2 can replay `grade()` in tools/puzzles/logic.js on the current marks (its techniques are cheap grid rules, about 100 lines) or port its techniques 1-4; tier 3 ("places one correct mark") only needs the stored solution.
- (P3-06) The colour category has no pictures (only the red circle is allow-listed); it is used only in bands C and D, where clues are text. P3-07 can show a colour swatch next to the word if wanted (with the word, never colour alone).
- (P3-06) `PUZ.logic` is read the same way as the other banks: inside functions, at run time (`var PUZ = PUZ || {}` at the top of the data file).
- (P3-05) The play top bar (74-teasers.js, `.bt-tt`) cuts a longer type name with an ellipsis when the timer shows: "MathDoku" reads "MathDok…" at 380x780 in bands C and D (band B has no timer, and 600 px and wider are fine). A framework change (a smaller title font under 400 px, or the level under the name only) would fix every long name. **Fixed** in P3-17.
- (P3-05) index.html is 1,487,116 bytes after this chunk (fail at 1,500,000). A11b (puzzle-type code into its own precached file) frees about 200 KB (the five 7x-bt files with this one); until it lands, a parallel chunk that adds more than about 12 KB of code will hit the limit at merge.
- (P2-17) The task note listed f among the letters that use the descender line; f is drawn as a tall letter on the base line instead, as Andika and ball-and-stick print draw it (and as the d-trace.js header already said: tails of g j p q y). If a descending f is wanted (some cursive-ready models), it is one stroke's points and the test's `DESC` list.
- (P2-17) The grown-ups skill table has one row per skill, so the Tracing row keeps the maths icon colour (`SK.trace.area`); the map, the round and the activity log use the level's area. A per-stop row would need a table change.
- (P2-17) Lowercase lines follow Andika's proportions (x-height 0.63 of the ascender: top 10, mid 34, base 75, desc 94) rather than the half-height middle line of school paper, so the strokes sit on the font letter the prompt card and the rest of the app show.
- (P2-17, seen while testing; not this chunk) Two smoke steps failed once each in three check.sh runs on a busy machine (other sessions were running smoke at the same time): the placement full run timed out waiting for the next question (8 s), and "P2-08 step hints: dec 380x780 step 1 of 3: does not fit" (over 18 px, a random decimal item). Both passed on the other runs; the second looks like a random item that is too tall for the step panel at 380x780.
- (P2-17) The sheets draw every stroke's arrows at once, so where one stroke runs back over another (r's arm up the stem, u's stem down its right side) the sheet shows crossed arrows; the board only ever shows the arrows of the stroke being traced.

- (P3-04) Names: the catalog id is `mdoku` (BRAIN_TEASERS 4), but the generator, bank file and global are `tools/puzzles/mathdoku.js`, `src/data/d-puz-mathdoku.js` and `PUZ.mathdoku`. P3-05 registers `BT.reg({id: 'mdoku', ...})` and reads `PUZ.mathdoku` (inside functions, at run time).
- (P3-04) Most bank puzzles are solved by csp.js propagation alone (1 search node: B 34 of 36, C 31, D 5x5 11, D 6x6 4), so in the easy levels the order comes from the grader's effort (roughly, how many cells need cage reasoning rather than a given or a last gap). If the levels feel uneven, `score()` in mathdoku.js is the one place to change, then rerun `node tools/puzzles/build-banks.js mathdoku`.
- (P3-04) The spec gives no grade limits per band, so the generator only requires that the grader can finish (no guessing). Band B is all grade 1-2 (6 puzzles need only last gaps); band D 6x6 reaches grade 4 (cage in a line) in 8 puzzles and 5 (naked pair) in 1. If band C should never need grade 4, add a highest grade to `BANDS` in mathdoku.js (like sudoku's tiers) and regenerate.
- (P3-04) Hint 2 in 5.2 ("6 with x in two cells: 1 and 6, or 2 and 3") is exactly the grader's cage enumeration; P3-05 can list a cage's number sets at run time cheaply (at most 6^4 tuples for a 4-cell cage) without any solver.
- **Fixed in P2-17** (per-level `lvArea` and `skArea`). (P2-16) Skill `trace` has `area: 'math'` (its only stop, trace 1, is in Tiny Town). P2-17 puts trace 2 and 3 in ABC Beach (reading): `SK.area` is per skill, so those map stops, the round's subject colour (`qSubj`) and the activity log would say math. P2-17 can give the skill a per-level area (for example an `areaOf(lv)` read where `area` is read) or two skill ids; the stop ids `trace2`/`trace3` should stay.
- (P2-16) `skill-trace` icon not drawn: `ICO_SK.trace = 'edit'` (the pencil, shared with spelling).
- (P2-16) Palm rejection uses the touch's contact width (PointerEvent width/height over 60 px) and one-finger-at-a-time; checked with CDP touch emulation only. Worth a try on a real tablet with a hand resting on the screen.
- (P4-06) Block Builder and Hill Climb (60-blocks.js, 62-hill.js) still paint `petE()` on their canvases, now for all four pets (the dragon already did). `PETS[k].s` (the stage emoji) is only used by `petE` for those games. A later games chunk could draw the rig into an offscreen image.
- (P4-06) Now unused: the emoji-pet CSS (`.petwrap .hat` / `.petwrap .toy` in 40-games.css, `.aura,.petbig.aura` in 20-base.css; `.petwrap` itself is still used) and `.rw-pete` (42-rewards.css). P4-17 can delete them.
- (P4-06) Bird and sea pal leave the rig's `extra` group empty (the bird's wings are its arms, so cheer lifts them; the sea pal's fins are on the head so they turn with it). The dino uses `extra` for its back plates. P4-07 hats sit in front of the dino's head plates, the bird's tuft and the sea pal's head fin; the anchors are set so a hat covers their roots.
- (P4-06) index.html was already over the 1.2 MB warning before this chunk (1,300,262 bytes at v97); the three pets add 18 KB (1,318,459). Still under the 1.5 MB limit.
- (P3-09) With a real type registered, the shot `bt-hub-empty` (tools/shots.js) is no longer empty: it shows Patterns. If the empty hub still needs a picture, the shot can `BT.unreg` every type first.
- (P3-09) Size and count together: a big shape drawn twice is smaller than a medium shape drawn once (each copy shares the tile), so in odd-one-out items with a 2-2 count split children must compare sizes within the same count. The colour often follows the size there, which helps; a later pass could keep size and count from varying in the same odd-one-out item for band A.
- (P3-09) Hint words of a 3-attribute item in band D run to three sentences (one rule per attribute); fine on tablets, two lines plus on phones.
- (P3-09) The Patterns card uses the existing `skill-pattern` icon; P4 may draw the 19 puzzle-type icons.
- (P3-10) The play top bar cannot show a long type name at 380 px when the timer chip is there (bands C and D): `.bt-tt` is a flex column, so `text-overflow: ellipsis` never applies and "Number Pyramid" was cut to "Number P" with no dots. P3-10 uses the short names "Pyramids" and "Target" (the hub cards have room for the long ones). A framework fix in 48-teasers.css (wrap the name in its own span with ellipsis, or a smaller title on phones) would let every type keep its catalog name. **Fixed** in P3-17 ("Number Pyramid", "Make the Number").
- (P3-10) The Magic Square (BRAIN_TEASERS 5.6, "part of the pyramid chunk", bands C-D) is not built. It could be a fourth type in 77-bt-number.js reusing the pyramid's keypad and brick look and `PYR.spread`-style line propagation for its check.
- (P3-10) The three types use the existing `blocks`, `scale` and `target` icons; P4 could draw a pyramid icon. `BT.ORDER` already places them in catalog order.
- (P3-08) Maze cells are smaller than the spec's 28 px on the largest mazes when the screen is small: an 18x18 maze is 19 px a cell at 380x780 and 22 px at 1024x600 (33 px at 600x960). The board is always the largest square that fits (BRAIN_TEASERS 8), and the drag steers the bear cell by cell toward the finger along open sides, so a finger never has to hit a 19 px cell exactly. A zoomed, scrolling board for phones would need a camera that follows the bear; not done.
- (P3-08) The maze's arrow-pad choice is stored as `S.bt.maze.pad` (1 or 0), beside the band records of the type (`S.bt.maze.A` ...). `BT.rec` and the star counts read only the band keys, so nothing else sees it. If P3-17 or P5 adds a real per-type settings place, move it there.
- (P3-08) The `maze` icon was added to `15-icons.js` (right after `puzzle`, so parallel chunks adding icons at the end do not conflict). The other 18 Brain Teaser type icons are still missing; their cards fall back to `puzzle`.
- (A11) index.html is now mostly code: about 929 KB of the 1,160 KB is `src/js`. The math worlds and puzzle types still to come add code, so the 1.2 MB warning will come back after a few more chunks. Next levers, in order: move data that still lives in `src/js` (word lists in 20-words, `SENT_*` in 32-gen-read, `SPN` in 34-gen-es, art drawings in 16-art-a) to `src/data` (no build change needed; check nothing reads them while loading); then, if needed, the games (60- to 72-, about 190 KB) as a third precached file added only when a game is opened, which would also cut start-up parse time. **Done in A11b (v105)** for the games and the puzzle types, as files loaded at start like the data file (not on demand: that would need fetch or script injection, and a game opened offline before its file was cached would fail). Moving data out of `src/js` is still open.
- (A11) `src/data/d-spell.js` holds code as well as words (`spellList`, `spellPic`, `genSpell`, `SPELL_CACHE`). It ships in the data file and works (SK.spell calls `genSpell` inside a closure), but it is the one data file that is not data only; moving the three functions to a `src/js` file would make the rule exact.
- (A11) The update guard text is English only, like the rest of the chrome.

- (P3-01 merge) The Brain Teasers card on Home is hidden until at least one puzzle type is registered (`BT.list()`), so a release between P3-01 and the first type (P3-03 sudoku) does not show children an empty hub. Smoke and shots register the fake type first, so they still see it.
- (P3-02) The human-style grader tries naked single before hidden single (the order BRAIN_TEASERS 6 lists), so almost every bank puzzle in bands B to D grades 2 (naked single); only 1 C hard and 4 D easy/medium puzzles grade 3. Both are allowed in those bands, so nothing is rejected, but P3-03's hint tier 2 should not assume a puzzle's grade names the only way to solve it: at each step it can look for a hidden single in a box first (children usually find those more easily) and fall back to a naked single.
- (P3-02) Band B easy (8 givens on 4x4) has 8 puzzles that need only "last empty cell" (grade 1), like band A. They are different puzzles from band A's (the duplicate check covers both bands), but if B should always need a single, set band B's lowest grade to 2 in `BANDS` (tools/puzzles/sudoku.js) and rerun `node tools/puzzles/build-banks.js sudoku`.
- (P3-02) Band D hard puzzles all need locked candidates (grade 4); none needs a naked pair after locked candidates have been tried, because locked candidates is tried first. The generator requires D hard to need grade 4 or 5 (so it is harder than D medium, which allows singles only); about 1 in 115 random 31-given symmetric puzzles qualifies, which still takes only about 4 s.
- (P3-02, seen while testing; not this chunk) smoke's `P2-21 grammar` block fails about 1 run in 5: the level 8 stop draws a random question, and "What kind of word is ...?" choices "preposition", "conjunction", "interjection" overflow their 168 px button at 380x780 (183-186 px of text). A probe of 40 draws found it in 10 on the unchanged v88 build and 6 with this chunk. Fix in 38-bank.css (let these choices wrap or shrink) or give the level 8 kind-of-word items the wide form. **Fixed** in "Fix: long answer words were cut off in two-column choices" (bank answers longer than 7 letters go one per row; level 8 word types are wide).
- (P3-02) `PUZ` is the shared global for every puzzle bank (`var PUZ = PUZ || {};` at the top of each `d-puz-*.js`). A `const PUZ` or `let PUZ` anywhere in src/js would be a redeclaration error for the whole script, so P3-01 should read `PUZ.sudoku` (inside functions, at run time) rather than declare it.

- (P2-26 merge) Grade 3 and grade 4 each had a near-identical "How Bees Make Honey" passage, and grade 3 and 4 both explained the Moon's phases. Grade 4's two were replaced at merge with "How Paper Is Made" (steps in order) and "Why We Have Seasons" (cause and effect). Grades 2 and 3 still share two topics at different depth (honey bees, the Moon's phases): R-01 should decide whether to keep that as spiral review or replace one. A test that fails on two passages with the same topic word in their titles would catch this next time.
- (P2-26) Passage stop titles are numbered in map order, like Sight Words: Read a Story / Learn About It (grade 2), then 2 to 5 for grades 3 to 6 (comp 4-7, info 2-5). Titles only; stars are keyed by stop id. Grade passages in d-comp.js are in grade order (none had shipped, so no saved review item points at a position yet); from now on append only.
- (P4-18) The drawn map costs about 1.4x the old one (stop icons through the sprite `<use>`, drawn boss art, star SVGs, labels on pills): best-of-40 unthrottled draw of a 16-world map went from about 3.2 ms to 4.5 ms; about 32 ms at 6x throttle on a quiet machine, under the 50 ms limit. The smoke map check now takes the second fastest of its 7 draws instead of the median, because with many checks in parallel the median measured the machine's load (it failed at 50-68 ms while the old build measured 25-56 ms beside it). If the map needs to be lighter on real tablets: inline the stop icons, or draw the neighbour worlds' stops without icons.
- (P2-23) Level 10 'order by strength' (CURRICULUM E-vocab) is a choice item ("Which word means the hottest?", four words from one set, the strongest or the weakest) because `BANKF.order` still has no builder. When one is registered, the 14 sets in `VOC_L10S` can become tile-order items.
- (P2-23) A word between `*stars*` in a bank prompt now prints bold (`bankTxt` in 37-bank.js; `bankSay` and the label strip the stars). Level 7 context clues use it for the hard word. The label is now cut to stay under 60 letters in all (the question part shrinks, the answer part is cut at 24), which does not change any label that was already short enough.
- (P2-23) Homophone items (level 6) always offer the sound-alike word plus two words that do not fit, so a child cannot pass by sound alone. Level 6 has 95 items about 45 sound-alike sets; level 8 mixes 26 idiom meanings with 33 which-kind items (simile, metaphor, personification, idiom); level 9 asks 24 roots and 38 words built from roots; level 10 has 36 analogies and 28 strongest/weakest items.
- (P2-22) The bank forms `order`, `match`, `multi` have no builder (`BANKF.*` is never registered), so level 3 compound words use choice items ("Which two words make rainbow?", "What do sun and flower make together?") instead of tile building. The first chunk that registers `BANKF.order` can add tile items to `BANK.vocab`.
- (P2-22) New optional bank field `em: [artName, emoji]` (a picture above the prompt through `picFor`; 37-bank.js, validated by `bankItemProblems`, class `.bem` in 38-bank.css). P2-23 and later content can use it. No skill icon was added to `ICO_SK` for `vocab` (it shows the reading-path icon); P4 icon work could add a `skill-vocab`.
- (P2-22) Level counts are 56, 52, 68, 63, 82 items; every level has more than the 40 CURRICULUM asks. smoke.js ran 4 full times on this branch with other agents' smokes running at the same time, slow but green.
- (P4-18) `.map svg` in 40-games.css pinned every svg inside the map (icons, labels) to the map's top-left; it is now `.map>svg` (the trail only). Anyone adding svg to map markup no longer needs a position reset (`.map .pet-svg{position:static}` is now redundant).
- (P4-18) The Game Room game-time card and rules card stretch the full width at 1024x600 while the game cards stop at 640 px; P4-16 can give `.gx-list` children one width.
- (P4-18) `GFX.card` puts `.pathcard` and the old `.game` class on one button (smoke selects `.game[data-s]`); 47-gfx.css restores the path-card grid over `.game`. When `.game` is retired (P4-17) the extra class can go.
- (P2-24) Adding a stop to the end of a world makes the world's boss wait for it: a child who passed every old stop of Space Station but has not beaten its boss finds the boss locked until they play `sent3` (the boss unlocks from the stop before it); the map then opens on `sent3`. Nothing passed is locked and no star moves; `tools/test.js` allows exactly this one case. Same for any later stop appended to a world. A rule such as "a boss opens when every stop that existed when it was last open is passed" would remove it; left for an engine chunk.
- (P2-24) One engine line changed: `curNode()` in 42-paths.js no longer falls back to a NEW stop (`!stopNew(p,i)` in the `any` branch). Before, a child who had finished the whole reading path opened the map on `sent3` (the new stop) instead of the last boss; now the map opens where it did.
- (P2-24) `sent` is still titled "Read & Match" in the skill table and the Grown-ups report (levels 3-5 are unscramble, cloze and story order); a better title is a copy change for a later pass. The three stops carry their own titles (Build Sentences, Fill the Gap, Sentence Stories).
- (P2-24) Smoke flakes seen while testing: the first versions of the `// P2-24` block twice scored 9/10 on the round that followed a quick second `start()` of the same stop (stale timers from the previous round); the block now returns to the map, waits, and goes Home between stops, and passed 3 runs in a row; separately one full run failed the existing `P4-05 tap: cheer face` step (not this chunk; a rerun passed).
- (P2-24) The word-tile unscramble proof is a role-pattern check, not a parser: each of the 40 sentences carries a role string and `tools/test.js` shows that no other order of its middle words fits the known statement or question shapes. A new sentence that needs a new shape has to add that shape to the test first (which is the point).
- (P2-08) Step hints exist now (`hint: stepsAdd(a, b)` etc., 39b-steps.js). Still on all-visible lists or plain text: Treasure Bay `sub 5`, `story 4`, `pv 2` (`BAY.steps` could return `stepsStory(list)`); old `big 3/4`, `sub 4` (blocked by the "old levels unchanged" JSON test, which also compares hints); spelling hint 2 (a timed show-the-word hint, not a step list). Decimal multiplication and division (dec 4-5) have no builder yet: multiply or divide as whole numbers with `stepsMul`/`stepsDiv` and add a last "place the point" step.
- (P2-08) The question card folds (`.qarea.hs-fold`) only when a step overflows it; the check runs in a `setTimeout(0)` after the panel is drawn, so a phone may show one unfolded frame first. 3x2 partial products show 6 parts plus the sum; at 380x780 the last two steps fold a and b away.
- (P2-04) smoke.js is flaky on existing blocks, not on Treasure Bay: the Shape Garden block failed in 2 of 4 full runs (`meas1` target 43 px; `waitForSelector('#prompt')` hidden at line ~1112, reproduced on the untouched v69 + tooling commit), and the P4-05 "cheer face" tap failed once. The Treasure Bay block passed in both runs that reached it. Shape Garden questions come from an unseeded `genQ`, so a bad draw (tap target under 48 px, empty text-hidden prompt) shows up sometimes.
- (P2-04) `VIZ.ruler` clamps a cm ruler to 6-15 (CURRICULUM M-2 says 0-20), so `meas 3` uses cm up to 15 and inches up to 12. A longer cm ruler needs a change in 23-viz.js.
- (P2-04) Multi-step hints (`BAY.steps`) are an `hsteps` list with every step visible at once (k:'html'); P2-08's stepper (`UI.hint` o.steps) can reveal them one per tap. At 380x780 a three-step hint under a vertical sum already fills the area above the number pad, so keep step text short.
- (P2-04) Placement: the math path now has 10 worlds; every placement expectation in test.js and smoke.js is computed from the world count and PLACE.MAX, so nothing changed, but the 5-testlet cap (see the P2-03 note) puts an all-right starter further from the end with each inserted world.
- (P4-03, found while testing; pre-existing) Smoke can fail "Shape Garden stop meas1": a `tap` question's crayon bar (`.sgt.sgbarrow`, a 150x24 SVG) is only 43 px tall, under the 48 px target. It is random: 22 of 60 fresh meas1 questions at v69 (built from 3ba01a4) have it, so smoke fails roughly one run in three. Fix in 31-pk.css (min-height 48 px on `.sgbarrow`) or in the meas1 generator; not touched here (content file, P2-02).
- (P4-03) Placement buttons ("Find my level" in Grown-ups and setup, `placeSubBtn` in 45-placement.js) still show `PATHS[p].e`; left for P4-12 (setup is shared with the placement screens). Use `ico(UI.SUBJ_ICON[p])`.
- (P4-03) The Stickers grid's empty slot (`.st.no`, 30-learn.css) still uses `filter: grayscale(1) brightness(1.6)` (old CSS, against CONTRIBUTING 7). The old filter made the emoji vanish; the new `question` icon now shows faintly through it, which reads better. P4-07 should drop the filter and style the empty slot with tokens.
- (P4-03) Planned but not drawn: `skill-dec`, `skill-alg`, `skill-gram`, `skill-vocab`, `skill-trace` (no such skills yet) and the 19 Brain Teaser puzzle-type icons (P3 not built). `icoSk()` gives an unknown skill its subject icon, so new content never shows a blank; the content chunk can add a picture to `ICO_SK`.
- (P4-03) The soft tone is `currentColor` at opacity .4, so on a `--brand-tint` chip a brand icon's soft parts are subtle by design (the primary layer carries the meaning). A screen that wants a coloured accent can set `--ic3` (for example `--ic3: var(--star)` on a dark icon).
- (P4-03) Game Room tiles now show two-tone icons in their tile colours (car in `--play-shade`, palette in `--read`, blocks in `--es`); P4-16 may put them on a white badge disc like the path cards.
- (P4-08, found while testing; also on v69) smoke.js is flaky in steps that do not touch Home: Shape Garden `pos1` sometimes renders a prompt 0 px wide (the `.sgscene` question, so `waitForSelector('#prompt')` times out after 30 s), Shape Garden `meas1` sometimes has a 43 px tap target, and "review in a stop round" / "tricky mix" sometimes count one more item fixed than asked. Reproduced with the v69 smoke against the v69 build; they depend on the random question. Question-screen owners (P4-10 / P2-02 follow-up) should look.
- (P4-08) `90-app.js` re-renders Home when the install prompt arrives only if `$('.hero')&&$('#map')`, which was never true (Home has no map) before or after P4-08, so the Install button appears only on the next Home render. Probably meant "on Home"; a one-line fix for whoever owns 90-app.js (Home's root is now `.scroll.hm`).
- (P4-08) Home no longer calls `revCard()` (59-review.js) or `placeBanner()`/`placeSubBtn` (45-placement.js): it draws its own Review tile and "Find my level" card with the same ids (`#revcard`, `#rev`, `#revn`, `#plcard`, `[data-place]`, `#plno`) and still wires them with `revCardBind()` / `placeBannerBind()`. `revCard()` is now used only by test.js; `placeSubBtn` still serves the Grown-ups row (with emoji). Tidy in P4-17/P4-18.
- (P4-08) Icons Home would use once P4-03 lands: `download` (Install button, uses `plus`), `timer` or `flag` (Speed Race, uses `bolt`; Fact Sprint uses `path-math` + a bolt badge), `shuffle` (Mixed Practice, drawn as three subject discs), `food`/`bath`/`ball` (pet thought bubble, uses the apple and ball drawings and drawn bubbles). The pet-tap burst (heart, sparkles, star emoji) is an effect, not chrome, and was left for P4-17.
- (P4-10) Old question-screen rules are now dead or overridden by `src/css/42-question.css`: in 30-learn.css `.track`, `.streak`, `.pad .k/.go/.del`, `.ch.right/.wrong`, `.lt`, `.ls`, `.slot`, `.fb`; in 40-games.css `.rtag`, `.hint.pop`, `.talking`, `.choices.wide/.syms .ch`; in 32-kinds-a/37-kinds-b the `.os/.ot/.mb/.sk/.mc` looks. P4-17 can delete them (Fact Sprint still uses `.pad .k`). The `#fb` element in shell.html is no longer used by `fb()`.
- (P4-10) Fact Sprint (57-fluency.js) builds its own old number pad (`.pad .btn.k`); it could call `padHTML('')` from 49-kinds.js to get the new keys. Speed Race keeps its old top bar (emoji chips); the placement quiz keeps its own top bar. Both get the new kinds.
- (P4-10) The boss bar inside the round top bar (boss emoji, red hp bar, heart emoji, `bossHit` emoji burst) is untouched for P4-11; on phones it moves to its own row like the track.
- (P4-10) The toast drops under the top bar on the question screen with `body:has(.qtop)`; a WebView without `:has` (Chrome before 105) shows it at the top as before.
- (P4-10) `node tools/shots.js question-read` fails before and after this chunk: `start('read', 5)` hits a stop whose skill is not in SK (TypeError reading 'round'). Point it at a real reading stop.
- (P4-10) Two smoke steps are flaky before and after this chunk: "review in a stop round" / "tricky mix" fail when a random old-stop question (q.review) has the same key as a due review item, so `revRight` books a third item (59-review.js counts any question by `revKey`); and the Shape Garden notext loop timed out when a pos1 depth scene was drawn, because `.sgdepth{width:min(100%,9em)}` collapsed to 0 inside a shrink-to-fit prompt with its words hidden. The second is fixed here (the prompt now spans the card); the first needs a fixed seed or a check that ignores `q.review` questions.
- (P4-10) Icons wanted from P4-03 for this screen: `undo` (the build Undo button uses `back`), `sparkle` (the right-answer burst uses small stars), `ear` or `listen` (Pre-K "listen" disc, today the emoji-free `speak`).
- (P2-07) When every other stop of the math path is passed (the `maxed` fixture), a forest that was finished before now opens the map on its first NEW stop (round1) instead of the last stop: `curNode`'s last resort offers the only unpassed stop. A child with any older stop open (midway) keeps that stop, and the twelve stops show NEW and stay open. Same rule as the P2-15 stops; test.js asserts both cases.
- (P2-07) A forest that is not finished changes under the child: the new stops sit in front of `frac3` to `frac5` and the boss, so a child who had passed all four fraction stops finds the boss locked until the twelve new stops are passed (no stars move). Expected for an unfinished world (same as sound3 in P2-15).
- (P2-07) Levels `sub 5`, `time 4`, `data 2`, `meas 3` are P2-04's (Treasure Bay). Until it ships they ask like the next level up (`ffSub6`, `ffTime5`, `ffData3`, `ffMeas4`) and are listed in test.js RESERVED (`sub:[5],time:[4],data:[2],meas:[3]`). P2-04 must write the real levels, delete them from RESERVED and merge its `SK.sub`, `SK.time`, `SK.data`, `SK.meas` lines with the dispatchers added here in 38-skills.js (one dispatcher per skill; levels 1-4 keep calling the old generators).
- (P2-07) The scale dial (g, kg) and the measuring jug (mL, L) of `meas 4` are two small inline SVGs written in 33-gen-math-mid.js (`ffDial`, `ffJug`), not VIZ functions, because 23-viz.js was outside the chunk. A later VIZ pass (P4) could move them there; the test reads the needle angle and the water level back from the markup to check the answer.
- (P2-07) `geo 1` polygons (`ffPoly`) are always drawn as a regular shape with the side lengths as labels, so the picture is not to scale (a 1 and a 20 side look equal); the rectangle uses VIZ.rectSides and gives two sides. Triangle sides always satisfy the triangle rule. A 4-sided figure is called "the shape with 4 sides" in the prompt.
- (P2-07) `shape 5` "How many right angles?" answers 4 for squares and rectangles and 0 for the other drawn shapes (the drawn triangle, rhombus, trapezoid, hexagon have none); there is no right-triangle picture in VIZ.shape. `data 3` bar graphs never use half steps (values are whole multiples of the scale, 1 to 5 lines high); half symbols appear only in picture graphs at scale 2 and 10.
- (P2-07) `time 5` picture choices (digital -> pick the clock) only offer clocks that differ by 5 minutes or more, or by the hour; the digital choices (clock -> time) also offer times one minute off. `frac 3`'s "later 0-2" lines were kept (decision left to P2-10 / a tablet look).
- (P2-07) Found, not fixed (P2-02's file `src/css/31-sg.css`): the "behind / in front of" variant of `pos 1` (class `sgdepth`, `width:min(100%,9em)`) lays out with a prompt that is 0 px wide in a 380x780 round (measured: #prompt width 0, height 262; the "above / beside / between" variants are fine). Its children are placed by `left:36%` / `left:30%` of that width, so the two animals probably sit at the left edge instead of the middle. The same thing makes Playwright call #prompt hidden, so the P2-02 smoke loop over Shape Garden stops fails in about one run in two (it hit 2 of 6 full runs of this chunk at `pos1`, and once on the baseline build). Give `.sgdepth` a real width (for example `width:9em;max-width:100%`) and look at it.
- (P2-07) Found, not fixed (P2-02 again): the same smoke loop fails when `meas 1` (Measure with Cubes) draws its shortest tower: a tap target of 43 px (limit 48; "min":43 in the failure). Measured by starting the stop 30 times: 8 of 30 starts have a target under 48 px on this build and 7 of 30 on the v69 build, so it is old and random; it failed 3 of the 6 full runs of this chunk. Together with the `pos1` width bug and the P5-02 review steps ('review in a stop round' and 'tricky mix' failed in 2 of 6 runs: a random question of the round matched a due item's label, see the P2-01 note) `check.sh` is red most of the time without any change in this chunk. Every P2-07 step passed in all 4 runs that reached it, and 1 of the 6 runs was fully green (smoke OK, run before the last cosmetic changes to the dial, the polygon labels and the clock captions).
- (ONLINE) Report card at 380x780: the stat label "Accuracy" breaks mid-word ("Accurac / y") in the 4-column stat row; P4 restyle of the report card (or a shorter label / hyphens:auto) should fix it.
- (P2-06) A placement quiz is capped at 20 questions (5 testlets) so a child starting at K can no longer be placed in the last math world; with 16 math worlds (CURRICULUM 2.1) an all-right child ends about 5 worlds up. Intended by P1-07, but P1-07's cap may need a second look once the path is full (grown-ups can still skip worlds).
- (P2-06) `frac` level 3 "later 0-2" lines are mixed in at 25% of the read-the-point questions (answers can be improper, like 5/4); P2-07 can drop that if grade 3 feels too early.
- (P2-18) `pic 5/6` hints name the letter pair ("Look for \"er\""), which narrows the answer; a gentler first hint could only say the word. er/ir/ur sound the same, so these levels test spelling memory, as CURRICULUM intends.
- (P2-18) Some level 6 pictures are generic emoji (down = arrow, new = badge, dawn = sunrise, noise = speaker); P4-13+ drawings for them would help. Several r-words (corn, fork, tiger, nurse) were left out because their emoji are not in the allowlist.
- (P2-02) `tools/shots.js` already had every `tiny-*` entry twice in the SCREENS object (a merge leftover; the later copy wins, harmless). Delete one copy in a tidy-up.
- (P2-02) Shape Garden is the first K world, so a K child with no stars now opens on it (Count to 20) instead of Number Meadow, and a grade-1 placement quiz starts there (first world with g >= 0). A child with stars anywhere further on is not sent back (`curNode`, see the entry). When P2-03 inserts Clock Tower between Candy Canyon and Ocean Reef the same rule covers it.
- (P2-02) `shape` now spans three levels in one skill and `pat`, `sort`, `pk_more`, `pk_count`, `pk_num`, `add`, `cnt` gained levels, so "easier" and "harder" (mastery) follow map order: easier(add 1) is now add 4 (Shape Garden), harder(add 4) is add 1. Tests compute this from NODES.
- (P2-02) The tipped scale in `meas 1` uses VIZ.balance with two invisible tap zones laid over its left and right halves (`.sgbal`); a child who taps the beam or the pivot still hits one of them. A later VIZ option for tap pans would be cleaner.
- (P2-02) Only two shape colours exist in the viz palette (`v1` blue, `v2` orange), so the K shape levels are blue or orange; the three Pre-K colour pictures (PK.shp) stay as they were.
- (P2-03) Placement ("Find my level") passes one world per 4-question testlet and stops after 5 testlets (PLACE.MAX). With Clock Tower the math path has 7 worlds, so a child who is right every time starting at world 1 now ends on Multiply Mountain after 5 sets but is no longer reported as "passed every world"; every further inserted world (Shape Garden, Treasure Bay, ...) makes this worse. A later chunk could jump two worlds after a perfect testlet, or raise PLACE.MAX. test.js and smoke now compute the expectation from the world count and PLACE.MAX.
- (P2-03) `mastNear` (harder/easier stop of the same skill) used path order. Clock Tower puts add 5 and add 6 before Ocean Reef's add 3, so path order would have made "harder than add 6" = add 3. It now goes by level (next higher / lower level of the same skill that has a stop). Nothing changes for skills whose levels already rise along the path. The mastery test's path-order invariant became a level invariant.
- (P2-03) Levels without a stop until P2-02 ships: `add 4`, `shape 2-3`, `meas 1` (K, P2-02). They are listed in test.js `RESERVED` and ask like their neighbour (add 4 like add 3, shape 2-3 like the Pre-K shape question, meas 1 like meas 2). P2-02 must replace them and delete its entries from `RESERVED`.
- (P2-03) `frac` has only level 1 (`max:1`), `pv` only level 1, `meas` max 2, `data` max 1. P2-04 adds `pv 2-3`, `meas 3`, `data 2` (new generator branches next to genPv1/genMeas/genData1; raise `max`); P2-06/P2-07 add `frac 2-8` (switch `SK.frac.gen` to a dispatcher; `genFrac1` stays level 1). Cut pictures for frac 1 are `CT.cut` in 31-gen-math-early.js (unequal and equal circles and rectangles), not VIZ.fracCircle (equal parts only).
- (P2-03) `src/css/40-games.css` still has an unused `.clock{...}` rule (genTime now draws VIZ.clock). Left alone (file is exempt from the hex guard and not in this chunk).
- (P2-03) The Help panel of meas 2 numbers the cubes 1..n, so the last number shows the answer. It is the counting aid on purpose; if a later review wants it hidden, number only up to n-1.
- (P4-05) Block Builder and Hill Climb (60-blocks.js, 62-hill.js) still paint `petE()` (the emoji) on their canvases, so a dragon child sees the emoji there. Out of scope (games); a later chunk could paint the rig there via an offscreen image of `petSVG`.
- (P4-05) Moods come from the Pet Care meters only: food < 30 hungry, clean or fun < 30 sleepy, all 80+ happy, else idle; cheer and oops are the tap / right-answer and wrong-answer / bath reactions. There is no "dirty" look (the Pet Care text still says "needs a bath"); a later chunk could add one as a seventh face.
- (P4-05) Owner feedback during the chunk: the P4-13 object-art style "looks like 1999 graphics". The dragon was therefore drawn with 3-4 tones per colour area (shade rim, base, translucent white highlight, dark accent / contact shadow), light from the top left, a pear-shaped body and big glinting eyes. This goes past DESIGN_SYSTEM 6.1/6.2 (two tones, 6 KB per pet): the dragon source is 6.3 KB and test.js allows 8 KB. DESIGN_SYSTEM section 6 is being rewritten by another agent; 6.2 should then list the helpers `petHi` / `petBlob`, the `--pc-*` roles and the anchors described at the top of `src/js/17-pets-svg.js`.
- (P4-05) The dragon's dark accent uses `--es-shade` (the only darker green token). If pets get more species colours (P4-06), dedicated pet tokens in 00-tokens.css may read better than borrowing subject colours.
- (P4-05) `.stage .petbig` (40-games.css) keeps its large top padding, which made room for the emoji hat; the rig draws the hat inside the drawing, so P4-12 can trim it. The home `.thought` bubble still bobs forever (pre-existing, against DS 8.1).
- (P4-05) The map pet is now placed by its `translate(-50%,-100%)` (the old box `bob` animation overrode that transform), so the drawn dragon stands beside the current stop. Emoji pets keep the old behaviour.
- (P4-04) Old and new component classes live side by side: `.card`, `.meter`, `.tile`, `.slot`, `.bubble`, `.node`, `.empty`, `.stars`, `.danger`, plain `.btn` still drive today's screens. Each screen chunk (P4-08, P4-10, P4-11, P4-12, P4-16) should switch to `.panel`, `.gauge`, `.piece`/`.socket`, `.speech`, `.mapnode`, `.blank`, `.stat`, `.btn.risk`, `.btn.soft`; P4-17 can then delete the old rules (`.chip` in 40-games is the colour-by-number palette and stays).
- (P4-04) `fb()` (the big centre pop-up) is unchanged; `UI.toast` is the DS toast. P4-10 should decide whether `fb()` calls `UI.toast`.
- (P4-04) Icons missing for components: no `clock` (the timer chip uses `bolt`), no `chevron` (the context panel rotates `play`), no `info` (the info toast uses `hint`). P4-03 could add them.
- (P4-04) Round dots fit about 12 questions on a phone top bar; longer rounds (placement, 20) should show a gauge instead.
- (P4-04) Component tokens were added to `00-tokens.css` in one block (`--ledge-soft`, `--danger-shade`, `--play-deep`, `--hi`, `--hi-0`, `--dim`, `--drop`); merge note if another branch also appends tokens there.
- (P4-04) tools/shots.js defines the twelve `tiny-*` entries twice (once before the P2-19 spelling group, once at the end of SCREENS); harmless (the second wins) but worth removing.
- (P4-13b) Object art now has its own 4-tone palette (`--ill-*` in 00-tokens.css, 13 hues x highlight/base/shade/deep, plus white and ink) and DESIGN_SYSTEM 6.5 has the new rules. Section 6.1 (characters: "two tones per colour area") still describes the old flat style; pets (P4-05), bosses (P4-11) and wearables/stickers (P4-07) should move to the same light direction and tone count, and can reuse the `--ill-*` tokens, so everything on one screen looks like one set.
- (P4-13b) Eyes and other ink details in object art now use `var(--a-k)` (the `.art` default fill) instead of `currentColor`, so a drawing inside a coloured button or white text no longer changes its eyes. This supersedes the P4-13 note below about details following `color`.
- (P4-13b) Chrome around questions still shows picture emoji (for example the gift at the end of the question progress bar). Once icons or ART cover them they could switch to `ico()` / `picArt('gift')` for one consistent look.
- (P4-13b) Sets B and C should copy the "art set A" check in tools/test.js with their own name list (same limits: 2,500 bytes, 40 elements, roles only); the token-mapping part already covers every role in 21-art.css.
- (merge P2-05) The full smoke run now takes about 12 minutes. A run that crosses midnight fails the P5-03 daily review counts (items moved to "due tomorrow" become due again). A later chunk could freeze the page clock in tools/lib/browser.js.
- (P2-19) Spelling hint 2 ("show the word for 2 seconds, then hide", CURRICULUM E-spell) is not built: `hintHTML` has no stepped hints. A later hint chunk can add it to `genSpell` as a second hint step.
- (P2-19) Story Summit, Dragon Library, Author Island and Legend Lab exist now with only a spell stop each (plus the boss). P2-20 to P2-29 should insert their stops in CURRICULUM 2.2 order; do not change the spell stop ids. Word Workshop (`r_workshop`, grade 2) between Space Station and Story Summit still has to be created by P2-18.
- (P2-19) Spell level 1 pictures come from the existing reading emoji lists (CVC, BLEND, MAGICE, TEAMS) by word; most of the 400 spelling words have no picture, so the blanked sentence is the visible clue. P4-13+ drawings reach them through picFor.
- (P4-13) Pictures are multi-coloured by design and ignore `color` (only eyes and details follow it); a dark mode or a high-contrast mode would need to redefine the `--a-*` roles in 21-art.css. The pig and nose are pale tints on a white page; check on a real tablet. The `bank` items still use `pic` (ico names), not `picFor` (see PRE-W5 note).
- (P2-15) `curNode`'s last-resort fallback (42-paths.js) no longer lands in an untouched world below the start world: a maxed K child (every stop done, no ABC Beach progress) would otherwise open the map on ABC Beach. Tiny Town (P2-01, math) is covered by the same line.
- (P2-15) Existing saves with Pre-K chosen in setup (v49+) now start in ABC Beach (first world with g >= -1); K and above never do. A K child who has stars in ABC Beach stays there (progress in a world is progress).
- (P2-15) Adding stops to the end of finished-or-open worlds (sound 3 in Letter Meadow, sound 4 and syl 2 in Candy Canyon) puts them before the boss: a child who had passed `sent1` but not the boss must now play `sound3` first (the stop shows NEW only after the boss is beaten). Expected, listed in test.js as "behind a new stop".
- (P2-15) abc 1/2 depend on the voice saying a letter name ("Find the big letter B."). Voices differ; the hint always prints the letter and a word ("B is for ball"). A recorded-voice clip per letter (existing clips feature) would be the fix if tablets read single letters badly.
- (P2-15) `syl` choices show the number plus that many stars (no clap icon or clap emoji is available: the clapping-hands emoji is not in the allowlist). A `clap` icon in 15-icons.js would be nicer.
- (P2-15) The `speak` icon (person with sound waves) is used on the big Hear button for abc and syl 2 instead of the older raw speaker emoji.

- (P5-02) Home "Tricky Mix (N)" now counts review items due today (`allMiss()` = `dueItems()`), not every item still in the system: after a child fixes items they leave the count until they are due again, and the button reads "Mixed Practice" when nothing is due. P5-03 replaces the card with "Today's review: N due".
- (P5-02) In challenge or support rounds (P1-08) `mastQ()` comes first in `makeQ`, so the review slots at questions 6 and 8 are taken by the adapted question; a fast all-right child sees due items at questions 2 and 4 only. Daily Review (P5-03) is where a backlog gets cleared.
- (P5-02) `P(sk).miss` stays as a small tally `{label: {tot, q: {label}}}` only because the Grown-ups "Tricky ones" cell (80-grown.js, being changed by P5-06) reads `m.q.label` and `m.tot`. P5-06 or a later cleanup can read `S.rev` / the tally and drop `q` from the tally. An older cached app (rule 9) that records a miss writes a whole question into the tally again; the new app overwrites it with `{label}` on that label's next miss (bounded at 40 per skill as before).
- (P5-02) Items moved from saves older than v38 have no seed: `revQ` tries 120 seeds for the same label once and keeps the one it finds (`seed: null` when none; then each review is a fresh question of that skill and level). Labels whose format changed since (midway's `3 + 4` against today's add labels) never match, so those items get fresh questions until they leave the system.
- (P5-05) Quitting a round mid-question adds no time for that question (only answered questions count), and games (Balloons, Race, Hill Climb, Art) are not logged; P5-06 can say "learning time". Sprint seconds do not add to `q`/`ok`, so Sprint does not change accuracy.
Things noticed while working that are outside the current chunk. One line each, with the chunk that noticed it.

- (P2-01) The smoke step "P5-02 tricky mix" failed once in about three runs with `fixed` 8 instead of 7 (and passed on the rerun): the 3 non-review questions of a Mixed Practice round are random, and one can match a due item's label, which `revRight` then books as fixed. The step should compare against the items actually asked (`G.q.rvk`), not a fixed 7.
- (P2-01) Tiny Town sort 1 shows 4 pictures although CURRICULUM 1.3 says "3 max for PK"; the M-PK table asks for 4 items (3 alike, 1 different), so the table won. `gradeCfg().choices` (3) is still unused by generators.
- (P2-01) The placement quiz for a K child now starts in Tiny Town (one grade below) and takes 5 testlets to pass all math worlds; if that feels long, start K at Number Meadow by making `PLACE.first` use `max(grade - 1, 0)` for children above Pre-K.
- (P2-01) Boss character and prize still come from the world index, so Tiny Town (index 0) shifts every other math world's boss and prize by one (cosmetic; see the P1-05 note). Pin a boss per world id in P4-09.
- (P2-01) The words on Tiny Town map stops ("Count to 5") are text; a Pre-K child taps the picture node, the node picture is the skill emoji. P4-09 (map redesign) can add spoken stop names.
- (P2-01) Pre-K sort and pat use colours from tokens (red, blue, green, yellow, purple, orange); shapes carry an outline, but a colour-blind child may still confuse red/green or orange/yellow in "which one is different" by colour. A later pass could add a pattern fill per colour.
- (P2-05) Levels `arr 1` and `story 4` belong to P2-04 (Treasure Bay) and have no stop yet. `SK.arr.max` is 2 and `SK.story.max` is 5, so levels `arr 1` and `story 4` exist: `arr 1` asks like `arr 2` and `story 4` like `story 5` (`genArr`, `genStoryAll` in 33-gen-math-mid.js) so the 3,000-sample fuzz stays valid. test.js "skills: every level is reachable" has a `RESERVED` list (`arr:[1]`, `story:[4]`); P2-04 must write the real levels and delete that list.
- (P2-05) Fact Sprint `mfact` / `dfact` sets unlock when `mfact 1` / `dfact 1` is passed; the facts then grow with the highest level passed (`fluLv`): level 1 key factors 0 1 2 5 10, 2 adds 3 4, 3 adds 6-9, 4 is all of 0-10. Divide facts use divisors 1-10 (never 0) and answers 1-10. They share one 60 s / 20 facts record each; no per-level records.
- (P2-05) Array questions' choice sentences (`5 × 2 = 10`) wrap onto two lines at 380 px (the button font is large); readable, a later polish could shrink choice text for long sentences. `✖️` is used as the icon of `mfact` and `mbig`; `÷` (plain text, not an emoji) is the icon of `dfact` and the Fact Sprint divide set because the heavy division sign is not in `tools/emoji-allow.txt`.
- (P2-05) Boss and prize for Multiply Mountain come from its world index (`BOSSES[4%4]` = Gloop, same as Number Meadow, prize `BPRIZE[4]`); see the P1-05 note about pinning boss characters per world.
- (P5-03) The Daily Review round has no filler: it holds only due items (1 to 10). Mixed Practice still starts with up to 7 due items, so the same items can be asked by either button; a later pass could make Mixed Practice skip due items or limit the card to once a day if that feels repetitive. The card shows the whole backlog ("21 due") although a round takes 10 at a time.
- (P5-03) The Mixed Practice button still uses a raw emoji and inline colours (old home code); P4-09/P4-10 home redesign should move it onto ico().

- (P1-07) The roadmap's "within one world in 95% of runs" holds when the true level is within about 2 grades of the grade picked (test.js: 97.9% of 2,000 simulated students, truth from 3 worlds below to 5 above the start world). With the truth anywhere on a 16-world path it is about 57%: 5 testlets can move at most 5 worlds. If later grade bands get long, a coarse first step (skip 2 worlds on 4 of 4) would widen the reach.
- (P1-07) Spanish has no placement: its worlds are vocabulary sets in an order (`byLevel`), not a difficulty ladder. `placePaths()` takes every path without `byLevel`, so a graded Discover path (P2-36) gets "Find my level" for free; check its world list makes sense as a ladder.
- (P1-07) A placement result is tied to the grade it was taken at (`S.place[p].g`): after a grade change in Grown-ups the maps start by grade again (the skips stay). Re-running "Find my level" makes it count again.
- (P1-07) Bank-based skills choose items by least-recently-shown once a round has been played (BANKLIVE), so in the app a placement question from a bank skill is not exactly the seed's question; the stop list and the result rules are still deterministic. Only tests rely on exact questions.
- (P1-07) smoke.js answers placement questions with the round helper `answer()`, which reads `G`, by pointing `G` at the quiz state `PL` for the tap. A later cleanup could pass the state into `answer()`.
- (P1-07) The home "Find my level" card hides for the session with "Not now" (not saved), and disappears for good once a math or reading stop is passed or a placement is done.
- (P5-06) The report card reads stored misses from `S.prog[sk].miss[label].q.label` for "Examples they missed"; if P5-02 changes that shape, `REP.data()` in 82-report.js (m.needs) is the one place to follow it. Examples are matched to the stop's level first, then any miss of the skill.
- (P5-06) "Where they are working" is the highest world grade with any progress or a placement skip (graded paths only; Spanish shows stops passed). It is not read from `S.place`. A finished path reads "one grade below the grade chosen" when the content ends below the chosen grade; fine today, revisit when worlds above 2nd exist.
- (P5-06) No `chart` icon exists yet (P4-03), so the Grown-ups entry uses `target`. Brain Teasers and Discover subjects fall in the "Other" subject label until their `area` names are added to `REP.AREA`. A skill missing from `REP.STRANDS` lands in "Other skills" (test.js fails when a skill has no strand).
- (P5-06) The map mastery badge from the P1-08 note is still not drawn.
- (P1-08) The roadmap mentions a small mastery badge on the map (`masteryState(nid)`); not drawn because `46-home.js` is not in the chunk's files. P4-09 (map redesign) or P5-06 can add it with one call per stop (`mastStops(p)` gives every stop's state in map order).
- (P1-08) `ptCalc` (58-playtime.js) is unchanged: "mastered stops earn less game time on replay" already holds through 3 stars (`prev>=3`). P5-09 could also count `masteryState(nid)=='mastered'`. Forgiven challenge misses lift only the star rating: the finish screen can read "7 out of 10" with 3 stars, the game-time accuracy and "Perfect score" use the real score, and coins and stars added to the total are the real first-try count.
- (P1-08, DONE in P5-02: challenge misses are not review items) A missed challenge question is stored by `addMiss` under its own (harder) level, so it can come back in Tricky Mix and when the child reaches that stop; P5-02 should decide whether challenge misses become review items.
- (P1-08) P1-07 (placement) and any other round that must leave no trace can set `G.nomast = true` (no mastery record, no adaptation). Rounds with a `pool` (boss, test out, mix, passages, placement) never adapt but do record first tries unless `nomast` is set.
- (P1-08) "Fast" is a fixed median of the first five answer times (12 s, 16 s for Pre-K and K, `MAST.FAST*`). Tune on real tablets; a per-stop baseline from `S.mast[nid].ms` is the obvious next step once there is data.
- (P1-08) S.mast worst case measured at 28,991 bytes for 400 stops (today's skill ids, n 999, 365 days, every stop mastered). If later skill ids get much longer, drop `md` or store `p` in thousandths to keep under 30 KB.

- (P1-03) The roadmap asks for a hidden dev skill `zz_demo` in `SK`. Not done on purpose: it would ship demo code in the app. Instead `tools/kinds-samples.js` holds 11 sample questions (all `sk:'zz_demo'`), and test.js, shots.js and smoke.js run them through the real engine (`G=newG({sk:'zz_demo',pool:[...]}); next()`). A later chunk that needs a real in-app demo can promote them.
- (P1-03) Smoke step 7 (number pad reveal) leaves the old round's 2.6 s `next` timer running; it fires into whatever `G` exists later (it skipped a question in step 8). Step 8 now goes home and waits 3 s first. Cleaner fix: `next()` could take the round it belongs to, or `back()`/`home()` could cancel pending round timers.
- (P1-03) `.act` (40-games.css, Art Studio) is a global class with `font-size:16px`; any new feature must not reuse short names like it. The number-pad slot highlight uses `.sl-on`.
- (P1-03) `check()` still does the `el.classList.add('wrong'); el.disabled=true` on the tapped element; kinds that submit from a button that must stay usable (multi's Check button, order, tap, match) pass `el=null` and rely on the `.inp` fallback for the star/burst position.
- (P1-02, FIXED in v42) Old bug, kept on purpose (the chunk must not change behaviour): after the second wrong try on a `num` question the slot shows the child's wrong number in the orange "answer" style, not the right one (the pad handler writes `st.typed` into `#slot` after `submit` has revealed the answer; same in Speed Race after a wrong "check" tap). Fix in `KIND.num.bind` (49-kinds.js): skip the slot write when `st.lock` became true during `submit`. The voice already says the right answer.
- (P1-04) The spell keyboard is QWERTY with 10 keys in the top row; at 380 px wide each key is about 34 px wide (48 px tall). The roadmap's "44 px min keys in portrait" cannot hold for 10 columns at 380; height and gaps are as large as fits. A later pass could offer a 2-row-per-half layout if tablets in portrait complain (they get about 56 px keys at 600).
- (P1-04) Spell hint: `hintHTML` k 'next' reads `G.built`/`q.tiles` (build only); spell questions must use `{k:'say', w, s}` (validate rejects 'next').
- (P1-04) `tap` html is shown inside `.prompt`, whose font size is the huge `--big`; generators wrap content in `.tpz` (fs-6) and use `.tp` (48 px) for targets. A number line of more than 7 ticks does not fit 380 px at 48 px each.
- (P1-04) smoke/shots drive the new kinds by putting a sample into `G.q` and calling `renderQ()`; stale `next()` timers from a previous sample hit the new round, so smoke waits 2.8 s between samples.
- (P1-02) Fact Sprint (`57-fluency.js`) builds its own number pad (no check key) instead of `KIND.num`; P4-10 (keypad redesign) should move it onto `KIND.num.render/bind` so all pads stay alike.
- (P1-02) `KIND.*.right/reveal` find the answer area by fixed ids (`#slot`, `#ls0..`, `.ch`), so only one question can be on screen at a time. Fine for rounds and races; a two-question layout would need a root element passed in.

- (P1-01, DONE in P5-02) `makeQ` in 50-round.js still reads `Math.random()<.3` to choose between a stored miss and a fresh question, and copies stored miss questions with `{...q}`; both are for P5-02 (spaced review), which should store `{sk,lv,seed}` and rebuild with `genQ`. Old stored misses have no `seed`.
- (P1-01) `makeQ` still declared an unused `s=SK[G.sk]` after the genQ change; removed in P1-02.
- (P0-01) `node tools/smoke.js` takes about 95 s because the bot waits for the app's own 1.2 s between questions. Fine for now; a later chunk could shorten it with a fake clock.
- (P0-01) `node tools/test.js` only prints and always exits 0 until P0-04 makes it a real check.
- (P0-01) `python3 tools/build.py` re-draws the icons with Pillow, so `icon-*.png` can show as modified in git after a rebuild even when nothing changed visually.
- (P0-02) CONTRIBUTING 3.2 says boot.js is lines 2438-2439 and the CSS split is "near 58 and 162"; used 58 and 163 (line 162 is a blank line kept with 30-learn.css). All JS ranges matched the real markers. 46-home.js (734) and 00-core.js (322) have no marker comment; cut by the documented line numbers.
- (P0-02) CONTRIBUTING 3.1/3.2 still describe the pre-split layout as "today"; a later docs pass could update the 3.1 text. `src/data/` holds only a `.gitkeep` until the first data file exists.
- (P4-01) Fredoka (display font) not added: no network in this environment to fetch a subset woff2, so display text uses Andika 700 (`--font-display` is the single switch point for later).
- (P4-01) `30-learn.css` (36 hex) and `40-games.css` (157 hex) still contain raw hex colours; the check.sh guard covers only 20-base.css and grows as P4 chunks migrate screens. Legacy aliases (`--bg`, `--blue`, `--green`, `--red`, `--muted`, `--card`, `--big`) remain until P4-17; old `--math`/`--read` were light tints and are now the DS base colours (`.tile.math/.read` use `-tint`).
- (P4-01) `.petbig`/`.pet.aura` still use `filter: drop-shadow` (old code, forbidden by CONTRIBUTING 7); to be removed with the pet rig (P4-05).
- (P4-01) `html.calm` rules exist but nothing sets the class yet; the Grown-ups toggle comes with a later chunk (P4-12 / P6-01).
- (P0-03) ROADMAP section 9 says a missing `S.v` means 2, but both folded migrations must still run on v30 saves (single-map saves have no `S.maps`; index-keyed maps have no `S.mapv`). Code treats a missing `S.v` as 1 and runs migrations 1 and 2, each guarded by its own marker; latest is 3. A later docs pass could align section 9.
- (P0-03) `migrate()` is called from `src/js/42-paths.js` (not 00-core.js) because the map migrations need `NODES`, which only exists there; no file before it reads `S` at load time.
- (P0-05) A save corrupt message is now shown in the App log (resolves the P0-03 note below). Round recovery only triggers when `G` is live and `#prompt` is on screen; errors in games (Hill Climb etc.) are logged but show no overlay.
- (P0-03) A corrupt save is kept in `starschool-v2-corrupt` and `SAVE_CORRUPT` is set, but nothing shows it yet; P0-05's App log should display it. `SAVE_FAILED` is shown in Grown-ups.
- (P0-03) `node tools/test.js` still always exits 0 except for the new migration block, which sets exit code 1 on failure; P0-04 should fold it into named checks.
- (P5-01) Speed Race (`54-race.js`) and Fact Sprint overlap (both are 60-second math facts); a later chunk could fold Race's math category into Fact Sprint. Their progress bar (`.prog` inside `.top`) also does not show at 380x780.
- (P5-01) Game time for Fact Sprint is once per set per day (tracked in `S.flu[set].paid`), and a new record always adds +1 min even on a later sprint the same day. P5-09 should review these amounts.
- (P5-01) `mul`/`div` sets: add entries to `FLU.SETS` in `57-fluency.js` (each has an `ok()` unlock test and a `gen()`), nothing else needed.
- (P0-03) A save file that is literally `{}` (not the same as no save) would still crash `PETS[S.pet]`; not handled (pre-existing).
- (P0-04) Known exception in `tools/test.js` KNOWN: the 9 Spanish level 2 skills (`es_greet`, `es_color`, `es_num`, `es_teen`, `es_animal`, `es_food`, `es_body`, `es_family`, `es_mix`) have no `say` on purpose (a picture asks "How do you say it in Spanish?"; speaking the word would give the answer away), so the "say present" check lists them. A later chunk could give them a `say` for the question text or add a flag for silent-on-purpose questions, then delete the KNOWN entry.
- (P0-04) The emoji allowlist was seeded with all 309 sequences found in `src/` at v33. 13 are newer than Unicode 11 and marked `# risky` (may be empty boxes on old Android): wheel, pickup truck, large blue and green squares, ice cube, kite, magic wand, pinata, ringed planet, coin, mirror, bucket, rock. The coin (used 20 times: 38-skills, 46-home, 48-shop) is the most visible; P4-02 or P4-13+ should replace these with SVG.
- (P0-04) The "risky" test only knows Unicode 12+ ranges from a table in `tools/test.js`; it is a safety net for new emoji, not a full emoji-version database. CSS `content:'\1F...'` escapes are not scanned (none exist today).
- (P0-04) `node tools/test.js` needs a built `index.html` for the size check (it fails with a message if missing); `check.sh` builds first. The size budget (warn 1.2 MB, fail 1.5 MB) lives only in `tools/test.js`.

- (P4-02) Coin icon swapped only in chrome chips/prices (home, shop, pet care, grown-ups). `🪙` remains in `38-skills.js` (Coins skill `e`, a map node picture; P4-03 skill icons), `50-round.js` (world-finished toast "+20 🪙"; left alone because P1-02 is refactoring that file), `52-balloons.js`, and games 60/62/72 (out of scope). The other 12 risky emoji are untouched.
- (P4-02) The `.stars` chip line in `46-home.js` (stars + coins) was edited for the coin icon; P5-04 also adds to the home stat row, so expect a one-line merge there.
- (P4-02) The icon sheet is built inside `tools/shots.js` (no dev screen in the app). Icons are drawn for the 24 grid with the recipe in the `15-icons.js` header; `close` and `times` are nearly the same shape on purpose (times is slightly smaller).
- (P1-10) `VIZ.clock` is not used by `genTime` yet: the old `clockSVG` (32-gen-read.js) has raw hex colours and a different look, and the chunk allows replacing it only if identical. The new clock keeps "short red hour hand, long blue minute hand", so the hint text still fits; P2-03 (Clock Tower) should switch `genTime` to `VIZ.clock` and delete `clockSVG` and the `.clock` rule in 40-games.css.
- (P1-10) Tap visuals wider than about 340 units shrink on a 380 px phone (max-width), so their targets drop under 48 px: a fraction bar with 7-8 tap parts, or a number line with more than 6 tap values. Generators for the `tap` kind should stay within that (documented in the 23-viz.js header); P1-03/P1-04 could add a check.
- (P1-10, DONE in P1-11) Number-line hops: `numLine({hops: [[from, to, label?]]})`.
- (P1-10) `VIZ.base10(999)` is 404 units wide, so on a 380 px phone it scales to about 0.88 (cells about 5 px). Readable; a later chunk could stack flats when n > 599 if it looks small on a real phone.

- (P1-11) `coordGrid` draws axis arrows only at the positive ends and its labels sit on the axes, over grid lines (readable, a little busy in four quadrants); `angle({proto})` labels 0 and 180 overlap the baseline. Polish in P4.
- (P1-11) `balance` strings pass behind the counters on a pan; fine at 380 px but a later pass could draw the pan on top. Sphere highlight looks like a small hole at tiny sizes.
- (P1-11) `coinSVG` prints the value with a cent sign in Andika; if a font ever lacks it, `o.face = false` plus the generator's own text is the fallback.
- (PRE-W5) Bank items name their picture with `pic` (an ICONS name, drawn with `ico()`); object nouns should move to `picFor` once ART exists so banks get the drawings too.
- (P2-25) The passage panel and four wide choices do not both fit a 380x780 phone at the old sizes (the question line scrolled out of the card). Passage questions now get class `bpass`: compact choices and a 24vh panel body in portrait. Longer grade 3-6 passages will scroll inside the panel; check the question line is still visible in the shots of each grade chunk.
- (P2-25) A round is 2 passages (10 questions) as CURRICULUM E-comp says. A child who gets a question wrong sees "Get 7 right to open the next stop" (n = 10); there is no per-passage pass mark.
- (P2-25) The stops sit at the end of Word Workshop today. CURRICULUM 2.2 puts comp 3 and info 1 after the grammar, vocabulary and sentence stops of P2-20/22/24: when those merge, keep comp 3 and info 1 last.
- (P2-25) `ev` sentences are not highlighted in the panel yet (CURRICULUM mentions "Look at this part again"); the hint quotes them instead. A later pass could mark the evidence sentences in `bankCtx`.
- (P1-09) The finish screen text "Get 7 right to open the next stop" is fixed; on a 5-question passage round the pass mark is 4 (`floor(n*.7+.01)`). Reword the message from `n` when a passage skill ships (P2-25).
- (P1-09, DONE in P5-02: items keep `bk`, `revQ` calls `bankRebuild`) Stored misses in `makeQ` (`{...pick(ms).q}`) keep `q.ctx` and `q.bk`, so a missed passage question comes back with its passage; P5-02 should rebuild with `bankRebuild(q.bk)` (or `genQ`) instead of copying.
- (P1-09) Forms `order`, `match`, `multi` are validated but never drawn until P1-03/P1-04 add the kinds and register `BANKF.order/match/multi=(item,id)=>question`; the data shape in CURRICULUM section 8 (`order`, `pairs`, `a` as a list) is what the builders receive.
- (P1-09) Passage rounds skip the "review an older stop at question 4 and 8" step of `makeQ` (the round is a fixed list). Boss, mix and test-out rounds use the skill's `gen`, which asks a single passage question with its panel.
- (P1-09) `tools/node_modules` is not in a fresh worktree; `check.sh` runs `npm install` itself, which needs the network.

- (P1-06) A setup that is skipped quickly keeps K (the default, highlighted) as the grade; there is no "not sure" button. P1-07 offers "Find my level" after setup, which is the answer for unsure parents.
- (P1-06) `gradeCfg().choices` (3 answer buttons for Pre-K) and `.multi` (multi-step items for 5th-6th) are defined but no generator reads them yet; only `n` (round length) and `auto` (speech) are used by the round engine. Boss, test-out and mix rounds stay 10 questions at every grade.
- (P1-06) Pre-K and K now read every prompt aloud even when "Read questions out loud" is off (ROADMAP 3.1 "forced"); the Grown-ups toggle still turns off the other spoken praise. A grown-up who wants a silent Pre-K would have to mute the tablet.
- (P1-06) Migration 4 infers the grade only from math and reading worlds (K, 1st or 2nd today). A child with progress only in Spanish gets K. Because K..2nd worlds open by grade, a midway-style save now has Candy Canyon and Coral Reef open at once (more open, never fewer).
- (P1-05) CURRICULUM 2.3 says the Spanish worlds have `g: -1` "so only order gates it", but the ROADMAP 3.2 rule (a world with `g <= S.grade` is open) would open every Spanish world for any child. Resolved with a path flag: `PATHS.es.byLevel = true` turns the grade rule off for that path (worlds open by order, progress, skip). The Discover path (P2-36) must pick one: graded (like math) or `byLevel`.
- (P1-05) Migrations 1 and 2 now read old index keys through the frozen table `MAP_V42` (00-core.js) instead of the live `NODES`. Output is identical today (test.js checks `MAP_V42` against today's maps), but with the old code, inserting Tiny Town (P2-01) would have silently moved every legacy index-keyed save's stars to the wrong stops. ROADMAP 9 rule 3 says migrations are never edited; this edit is behaviour-preserving and the only safe fix.
- (P1-05) Boss character and boss prize still come from the world index (`BOSSES[wi%4]`, `BPRIZE[(pi*4+wi)%12]` in 42-paths.js), so inserting a world changes which boss and prize a world shows (cosmetic; owned prizes stay owned and the stars are keyed by world id). P2-01 or P4-09 could pin a boss per world id.
- (P1-05) Migration 3 copies `boss<k>` to `boss_<worldId>` and `math0` to `m_meadow` and keeps the old keys, so an older cached app on an offline tablet still reads the save (rule 9). A much later compaction migration may drop them.
- (P1-05) The v42 lock rule did not open a passed stop whose previous stop was unpassed (possible only in v30 single-map saves). Passed stops are now always open, so `legacy-single-map` shows 3 more open read stops (more open, never fewer). midway, maxed and fresh are identical stop for stop.
- (P1-05) `startMix` (50-round.js) calls `curNode(p)` once per stop (quadratic). Fine at 150 stops; hoist it if the mix screen ever feels slow.
- (P1-05) Map draw with 16 fake worlds (156 stops) at 6x CPU throttle: median about 29 ms (limit 50), single runs up to about 49 ms. The strip recomputes `worldOpen`/`worldFinished` per chip and `unlocked` scans the world; a memo per draw is the next step if P4-09 adds weight.
- (P4-11) The boss bar in the round's top bar (renderQ, P4-10's area) still shows hearts as emoji (`'❤️'.repeat(G.hp)`, also set by `bossHit`), the zap toasts (`ZAP! ⚡`...) and `burst()` / `confetti()` particles are still emoji, and `bossKO` still calls `confetti(40)`. P4-11 only changed what goes in `.bossE`: `G.boss.e` now holds the drawn boss (SVG markup, `RW.boss(name, 'idle')`), so P4-10 must not put `G.boss.e` into text or an attribute. A drawn heart / zap icon and drawn particles belong to P4-10 or P4-17.
- (P4-11) The map's boss node (`nInfo`, 42-paths.js) and the Pet Shop "boss" hats still show the boss and prize emoji; P4-09 can use `RW.boss(n.boss[1], 'idle')` for the node, P4-07 can draw the prizes (they stay emoji keys in `S.owned` / `S.wear.hat`, so a drawing must be looked up by that key).
- (P4-11) At 380x780 a finish screen with many cards is taller than the screen: hero, chest and the first cards show, the rest scrolls under the sticky dock (the next-step button is always visible). If children miss the later cards, a "collapse earned list" or two-step reveal is a P4-17 idea.
- (P4-11) Pre-existing flaky smoke step (P2-02): "Shape Garden stop meas1" measures `.ch, #prompt [data-v], .k` and some random `meas 1` questions give a 43 px minimum at 380x780 (measured 40 starts: 43 px in about a third, on the v69 build as on v75), so smoke fails on that step by chance. Either the tap zones of that question need 48 px or the step should measure only the tap zones' real hit size.
- (P4-11) Icons wanted from P4-03: `balloon` (Bonus game, today `gamepad`), `sparkle` / `confetti`, `unlock` (open padlock for "Next stop unlocked", today `map`), `zap` (a lightning with a spark for boss hits; today `bolt`), `sticker` (single sticker for the prize row), `medal` (the prize rosette as an icon), `heart-half` (boss life bar), `chest` (the treasure chest at icon size, e.g. for the round track where renderQ shows the gift emoji).

## Log

### P4-07 Drawn wearables, Pet Shop and Stickers (v121, 2026-11-11, Claude)
- What changed (DESIGN_SYSTEM 6.4, 9 row 7; the owner asked for no flat look anywhere): every wearable is drawn in the pets' style instead of an emoji at the anchors, and the Pet Shop and the sticker book are redrawn in the component look. DESIGN_SYSTEM 6.4 now has an "as built" part.
- Wearables (25): hats cap, bow, top hat, rescue helmet, grad cap, crown; toys balloon, lollipop, ball, teddy, kite, magic wand, trophy; the 12 boss prizes (daisy, star, party popper, planet, rainbow, fire, gem, clover, moon, lightning, donut, music) as badge hats: a headband in the prize's colour with a gold rosette and the prize's drawn emblem on its white face. Style: shade / base / highlight per area (a `wVol` helper draws the shape in the shade tone, then shrunk and nudged to the top left in the base tone), light from the top left, a soft dark contact accent where a hat meets the head, roles `var(--a-x)` only (21-art.css now also defines them on `.pw-hat` / `.pw-toy`), no outlines, gradients, filters, ids, text or `<use>`. The boss intro's prize rosette (`RW.badge`) now shows the drawn emblem too.
- Saves: no format change. The drawings are keyed by the emoji ids the save already stores (`S.owned`, `S.wear.hat`, `S.wear.toy`, `BPRIZE`); `wearOf(id)` gives petSVG the drawing (or the id, so an unknown id still shows its emoji). `petRigHTML` (Home, Pet Care, map, round runner, growth overlay) and `RW.pet` pass `wearOf`; `petSVG`'s contract is unchanged.
- Fit: the hat anchors were lowered onto the head and scaled up (hats .78-.98 of the 64 grid, toys .74-.98; were .68-.9 and .58-.78) for all 4 pets x 4 stages, so hats are worn instead of perched and toys read; every item on every pet at every stage (500 combinations) stays inside the 200 x 200 viewBox.
- Pet Shop (`shop()` in 48-shop.js, new `src/css/43-shop.css`): top bar (back, bag badge, coin chip `#coinc`); a sticky stage with the pet on its place (Home's drawn scene `hmScene`, replacing the emoji decorations and raw-colour gradient there) wearing what the child tries on; an info bar (picture, name, price or "It is yours", one action `#psact`: Buy / Put it on / Take it off / Need N more); tabs Hats / Toys / Places (`.tab[data-t]`, a segmented strip); cards (`.item.ps-card[data-id]`, `.own`, `.wear`, `.sel`) with the drawing on a soft well, the name, a coin price chip or a Yours / On / Prize tag; places show their drawn scene. Owned items go on and off with one tap as before; an item the child does not own is tried on with a tap, Buy opens a sheet ("Buy the crown? It costs 60 coins. You will have 4 left." `#psyes` Yes, buy it / `#psno` Not now); after buying it is worn, coins drop, drawn confetti (`RW.burst`) instead of emoji confetti. Not enough coins: a toast "Need N more coins! Finish a round to earn some." and no sheet. A worn item counts as owned in the shop (the hand-made fixtures wear a top hat they never bought). Wide landscape: stage left, shelf right. `stageHTML` stays for Pet Care (P4-12's screen).
- Sticker book (`book()` in 46-home.js): top bar with a sticker count chip, a header card (progress meter, how to win stickers, the P3-17 "Puzzle badges" button `#bkbdg`, kept), then 4 pages of 12 on cream paper with a ring binding (2 pages side by side from 560 px). A collected sticker is its emoji (collectible content, STK unchanged) cut out die-cut style: a white border that follows the picture (stacked text shadows), a soft drop shadow, a slight turn, a "x2" pill. Empty slots are soft cream squares with a faint star; the old `.st.no` filter (P4-03 note) and the old `.sgrid` / `.st` rules are gone (30-learn.css), as is `.item.own/.wear` (40-games.css; `.tabs`, `.shopgrid`, `.item` stay for the games and Pet Care). Tapping a sticker bounces it with drawn confetti instead of emoji particles.
