### P2-21 Grammar B, levels 6-9 (v82, 2026-11-11, Claude)
- What changed: `BANK.gram` in `src/data/d-gram.js` grows by levels 6-9 (219 items, same `GR(...)` pattern, every item has a topic, a hint that teaches the method without the answer, one right answer): 6 pronouns, possessives and look-alike words (64: its/it's, their/there/they're, your/you're, to/too/two, whose/who's, pronoun matches noun, possessives), 7 commas and quotation marks (41, all wide: series, dates and places, introductory words and names, comma before and/but/so, dialogue), 8 more parts of speech (57: tap the adverb, preposition, conjunction or interjection, adverb cloze, name the word type, has/have/had perfect tenses), 9 sentence craft (57: complete vs fragment, run-on fixes, combine two sentences, I/me, he/him, we/us, who/whom, vague pronouns). `SK.gram.max` is 9. Labels are the question plus the answer and must be under 60 characters, so level 7 and 9 questions are short ("Which is correct?") and the sentences are the answers.
- Stops, by world id: `gram 6` and `gram 7` end Dragon Library (`r_library`, after spell 5), `gram 8` ends Author Island (`r_island`, after spell 6), `gram 9` ends Legend Lab (`r_legend`, after spell 7). CURRICULUM 2.2 puts vocab 6-8 between them; those chunks (P2-23) insert their stops at merge. Stop ids `gram6` ... `gram9` are new; no existing id changed.
- Edits to existing files: `src/js/38-skills.js` (max 9), `src/js/42-paths.js` (four nodes), `tools/build.py` (v82, provisional), tools (test, smoke, shots). Expectations changed in P2-20's test (it described the whole bank, so it no longer holds): `SK.gram.max` is now checked as at least 5 (P2-21's test checks exactly 9), the "no item above level 5" line is gone (P2-21 checks level 9), and its stop-order string covers gram levels 1-5 only; P2-20's smoke block likewise lists only gram levels 1-5 stops. No P2-20 data or counts changed.
- Save safety: stars are keyed by stop id. The P2-21 test compares the reading path without gram 6-9 against today's over 6 fixtures x 7 grades (2,520 stop checks): no stop moves stars, nothing re-locks, a passed stop is never locked, the new stops in a finished world show NEW, are open and are never the current stop; plus a synthetic child who finished Dragon Library and Author Island.
- Tests: test.js check "grammar B P2-21" (bankProblems with min 40 on all 9 levels, exact counts, hints never hold the answer or a one-word wrong choice, 3,000 seeded questions per level = 12,000 with kindProblems zero, 2-4 distinct choices, one right choice, say, labels unique, every item drawn, reproducible, rebuild, forms per level, stop order ignoring other skills' stops). smoke.js: one P2-21 block (stops by world id; levels 6-9 at 380x780 including a forced tap and a forced choice question at level 8: buttons 48 px or more, no sideways scroll, hint teaches; one 10/10 round of level 7 saves stars and moves no other stop).
- Screenshots (380x780, tools/out/): gram-6, gram-7, gram-7b (four wide choices), gram-8, gram-8b (tap a word), gram-9 looked at. `node tools/shots.js gram-6 gram-7 gram-7b gram-8 gram-8b gram-9`.
- Note for P2-23: add vocab 6-8 to `r_library` and `r_island` by inserting `['vocab',6,..]` after `gram 6`, `vocab 7` / `gram 7`, `vocab 8` per CURRICULUM 2.2; the P2-21 test only looks at gram stops and their order against spell, so it keeps passing.
- Merge: built on a parallel branch, renumbered v81 -> v82 at merge.

### P2-24 Sentences: unscramble and cloze (v79, 2026-11-11, Claude)
- What changed (CURRICULUM E-sent): skill `sent` grows from 2 to 5 levels; levels 1-2 are untouched (the skill's `gen` is `lv=>lv>=3?genSentB(lv):genSent(lv)`). Level 4 "Fill the Gap": 40 cloze sentences, 3 same-part-of-speech words (nouns, verbs, adjectives, is/are/am, past forms), one blank shown as the slot. Level 5 "Sentence Stories": half `choice wide` "which sentence tells about the pictures" (two pictures through `picFor`, three sentences, only one names both), half `order` of 12 three-sentence stories (cue words or a thing that must happen first). Hints, `say` text and unique labels on every level. No new kinds.

### P2-25 Comprehension framework and 2nd grade passages (v80, 2026-11-11, Claude)
- What changed: new `src/js/37-comp.js` (the framework) and `src/data/d-comp.js` (the passages). 10 original grade 2 passages (5 stories, 5 nonfiction), 5 questions each = 50 questions, each of the types CURRICULUM E-comp lists for grade 2: detail, sequence, vocabulary in context, main idea, inference. The `comp` skill (Story Time) gets level 3 (stories) and a new `info` skill (Learn About It, level 1, nonfiction). Levels 1 and 2 of `comp` are the old picture stories, unchanged (same question for the same seed, tested). A round is 2 passages x 5 questions in order (10 questions; 3 stars at 10, 2 at 8, 1 at 7); the passage panel (P1-09) stays above every question; the hint quotes the evidence sentence(s).
- The framework (how P2-26 to P2-29 add a grade; header of 37-comp.js has it too): in `d-comp.js`, append `COMPSPEC[g]={words:[min,max], sent, maxSent, fk, need:[types]}` and `compAdd([{id:'g3f01', g:3, type:'f'|'n', title, text:[sentences], qs:[{t, q, a, d:[3], ev:[sentence indexes]}]}])`. `compAdd` makes the PASS items (`PASS.comp` lv = grade+1, `PASS.info` lv = grade-1; comp 3-7, info 1-5), keeps the authored copy in `COMP`, makes the hint (quotes `ev`) and the label (`title #n type`). `compProblems(g)` is the validator (10 passages, word count, average and longest sentence, Flesch-Kincaid grade, 5 questions with the required types, `ev` in range, answer words of detail and sequence questions are in the passage and in the evidence sentences, no wrong choice of a detail question is also supported by the evidence, a vocabulary question quotes a word that is in its evidence sentence, the right answer is not the longest choice in more than half the questions, PASS matches COMP). The test check loops over every grade that has passages, so a new grade is covered once its COMPSPEC row exists. Then, outside the data file: raise `SK.comp.max` / `SK.info.max` in 38-skills.js and add `['comp',lv,title],['info',lv,title]` to the grade's world in 42-paths.js (CURRICULUM 2.2). Passages are only ever appended (saved review items remember a passage by its position in PASS, `q.bk.bp`).
- Stops: `comp 3` ("Read a Story") and `info 1` ("Learn About It") are the last two stops of `r_workshop` (Word Workshop), after sight 5, vowel teams 2 and build vowel teams 2, before the boss. CURRICULUM 2.2 puts them after gram 2, vocab 2, sent 4, vocab 3, gram 3, sent 5 (P2-20, 22, 24); whoever merges those keeps comp 3 and info 1 at the end of the list.
- Edits to existing files: `38-skills.js` (comp line: max 3, `genCompLv`, `round`; new `info` line), `42-paths.js` (two stops appended to r_workshop), `15-icons.js` (`info:'doc'` in ICO_SK), `50-round.js` `start()` (a skill's `round()` may return null for "ordinary round"; needed because comp levels 1 and 2 have no passage), `37-bank.js` (optional `label` on an item, long prompts; passage questions get class `bpass`), `38-bank.css` (compact choices and a shorter panel under `bpass` so the question line stays visible on a 380 px phone; before this a four-row wide choice list pushed the question out of view), tools (test.js, smoke.js, shots.js), `tools/test.js` P2-18 check expects `comp3 info1` after build6.
- Save safety: stars are keyed by stop id (`comp3`, `info1`), no migration, `LATEST` unchanged. The new stops are appended at the end of an existing world: a child who had finished Word Workshop sees both with the NEW tag, open, and the map does not open on them; passing them changes no other key. Check compares every old stop (stars, passed, open) with a map without the two stops over 6 fixtures x 6 grades (1950 comparisons), plus a built "finished Word Workshop" save at grades 2, 3, 6: 0 stars moved, 0 stops re-locked, current stop moved on 0 of 36 loads. Only a child in the middle of Word Workshop who has done every old stop but not the boss is moved on, to the new first stop (by design: it is the next stop).
- Reading level: words per passage 64-77 (CURRICULUM 60-90), at most 9.6 words per sentence on average and 15 in any sentence, Flesch-Kincaid 0.8-2.1 (limit 3.5), no word the BAD filter catches, no real brands or people.
- Tests: test.js 2 new checks (data and rounds; stops and saves) incl. 10 validator trials proved to catch bad passages (ev out of range, answer not in the text, too long, a sentence too long, wrong question count, missing type, a wrong choice also supported, a vocab word not in the evidence, unknown type, reading grade), 3,000 questions each for comp 3 and info 1, 50 distinct questions, bankRebuild, live rounds reach all 5 stories in 3 rounds. Smoke `// P2-25` (380x780): the map opens on comp3 for a child who did the old stops, both stops played with real taps (10 questions each), panel hides and shows, hint, 48 px targets, no sideways scroll, stars saved, old stops untouched.
- check.sh: build, contrast (92 pairs), hex guard, test.js 53 of 53, smoke OK (no console errors, no network); index.html 985,126 bytes.
- Screenshots checked (380x780): story-map, story-comp (passage panel open, question line and four choices visible), story-info, story-hint, story-closed.
- Checklist 7.1: all ticked (midway fixture and no-network covered by smoke; no new emoji (the info skill uses the allowed magnifier); no raw hex in new CSS).
- Files: src/js/37-comp.js (new), src/data/d-comp.js (new), src/js/37-bank.js, 38-skills.js, 42-paths.js, 15-icons.js, 50-round.js, src/css/38-bank.css, tools/test.js, smoke.js, shots.js, build.py (VER v80, provisional), docs/PROGRESS.md, built output.
- Merge: built on a parallel branch, renumbered v79 -> v80 at merge.

- Math visuals (src/js/23-viz.js, src/css/32-viz.css; header documents the "Depth" rules): light from the top left like the object art. Counters (ten frames, dots, dice, arrays, balance, line plots, pictographs, number-line marks, grid points) are drawn in the shade tone with a deep rim; `vol()` adds a base-tone spot moved toward the light (a shade crescent shows lower right) and a short white gloss, as zero-length round-capped strokes, so each counter is still exactly one `<circle>`. Arrays do the same with shifted dashed paths. Ten frames, dice faces, fraction bars and circles, the clock and bills sit on a soft `--ledge-soft` ledge; fraction bars and graph bars get a white gloss band, fraction circles, the clock rim and coins a gloss arc; the clock face a lower-right crescent; base-ten blocks a deep-tone ledge; coins are metallic (grey or tan base, shade crescent, gloss, darker rim; penny text `--ill-brown-3`). Tokens only (`--ill-*` for deep tones), no gradients, filters or ids, deterministic; decorations have `pointer-events: none`, so tap targets, `data-v` and the 48-unit tap rule are unchanged. Typical sizes stay under 2.5 KB (tenFrame 20 with two colours 1.9 KB, base10(999) 2.2 KB).

Every item has a hint, topic, say text and a unique label; distractors are real mistakes (a synonym for an opposite, other prefix or suffix meanings of the same base word).

### P2-20 Grammar A, levels 1-5 (v77, 2026-11-11, Claude)
- What changed: bank skill `gram` (levels 1-5) in the new `src/data/d-gram.js`, CURRICULUM E-gram: 1 capitals and end marks (52 items: "which sentence is correct" wide, pick `.` `?` `!`, tap the word that needs a capital), 2 nouns, verbs, adjectives (60: tap the word in a 4-word sentence, which word is a noun/verb/adjective), 3 plurals (46: -s, -es, y to ies, f to ves, irregular; wrong forms are the real mistakes: mouses, foots, childs), 4 verb tense (51: regular and irregular past, present, future, which sentence is past/now/future), 5 subject-verb agreement and contractions (46: is/are, was/were, has/have, run/runs; don't, can't, it's, we're ...). 255 items, each with a topic and a hint that teaches the method without the answer; a say line (tap items also speak the sentence).
- Stops, by world id: `gram 1` ends Ocean Reef (`r_reef`, after spell 2); `gram 2` and `gram 3` end Word Workshop (`r_workshop`, after build 6); `gram 4` and `gram 5` end Story Summit (`r_summit`, after spell 4). CURRICULUM 2.2 puts vocab 1 before gram 1 and vocab 2/3, sent 4/5 between gram 2 and 3 (and vocab 4 between gram 4 and 5); those chunks are not in this branch, so they slot in between when merged. Nothing in the data depends on the order.
- Edits to existing files: `src/js/38-skills.js` (`SK.gram`, one group at the end, max 5), `src/js/42-paths.js` (five nodes), `tools/build.py` (v77), `tools/test.js` (new check; one expectation changed: P2-18's `r_workshop stops` was the exact string `sight5 pic6 build6`, now a prefix, since the world grows), `tools/smoke.js` (`// P2-20` block), `tools/shots.js` (gram-1 to gram-5, gram-1b, gram-hint). `src/js/37-bank.js`, `82-report.js` (strand Grammar already lists `gram`) and the icon table (falls back to the reading icon) are untouched.
- Save safety: stars are keyed by stop id (`gram1` ...), new ids; no existing id changed. In test.js the reading path without the gram stops is compared with today's over 6 fixtures x 7 grades (2100 stop checks): every stop keeps its stars, nothing re-locks, a passed stop is never locked, the map opens on the same stop. A gram stop in a finished world is NEW, open, has no stars and is never the current stop; a synthetic save that finished Ocean Reef, Word Workshop and Story Summit keeps all stars. Smoke: a round of gram 2 earns 3 stars and no other stop's stars move.
- Tests: test.js 52 of 52 (new check "grammar P2-20": bankProblems with min 40 per level, exact item counts, a topic and teaching hint on every item, hint never holds the answer, 3,000 seeded questions per level = 15,000 with kindProblems zero, 2-4 unique choices with exactly one right, say, unique label per item (under 60 chars), every item drawn, no BAD words, reproducible and `bankRebuild` identical, map stops, saves). smoke.js OK with a new `// P2-20` block at 380x780: five levels, answer buttons 48 px or more, no sideways scroll, hint check, one full round.
- Note for P2-21: `GR(lv, topic, hint, form, rows)` in d-gram.js builds items; append levels 6-9 there and raise `SK.gram.max` to 9. The bank engine builds the label from the first 60 characters of the question plus the answer, which must stay under 60 in all (the generator test enforces it), so keep questions short; tap items may have at most 4 words (the generator test allows at most 4 choices); the validator compares answers case-blind, so a "missing capital" wrong answer must differ from the right one in more than the capital.
- Screenshots (380x780, tools/out/): gram-1, gram-1b, gram-2, gram-4, gram-hint looked at.

### P2-07 3rd: world Fraction Forest (v76, 2026-11-11, Claude)
- What changed: the other twelve stops of CURRICULUM M-3b are in Fraction Forest (`m_frac`), in CURRICULUM 2.1 order with the four P2-06 ids kept: frac2, round1 (Round to 10), frac3, sub6 (3-Digit Take Away), geo1 (Perimeter), frac4, round2 (Round to 100), time5 (Time to the Minute), geo2 (Area), frac5, sub7 (Take Away Across Zeros), data3 (Scaled Graphs), shape5 (Shape Families), time6 (How Much Time Passed), meas4 (Rulers & Scales), story6 (Story Problems 6), then the boss: 16 stops + boss. New skills `round` (2 levels, icon target) and `geo` (2 levels, icon triangle ruler); new levels `sub 6-7`, `time 5-6`, `shape 5`, `data 3`, `meas 4`, `story 6`. All generators are in one block at the end of `src/js/33-gen-math-mid.js` headed `// ---- P2-07 Fraction Forest (3rd) ----` (names start `ff`; seeded randomness only, no new question kinds). Existing levels are untouched: each existing skill got a level dispatcher in `38-skills.js` (`lv<=old max` calls the old generator).
- Levels: round 1 nearest 10 (2- and 3-digit, number-line hint between the two tens), round 2 nearest 100 (3- and 4-digit); sub 6 vertical 3-digit with regrouping in one or two places, no zeros on top; sub 7 across zeros (X00 with a ones digit, X0Y, 1,000); time 5 analog to the minute -> 4 digital times, half reversed (digital -> pick one of 4 clocks); time 6 elapsed time from two clocks or two digital times (15 to 120 minutes) or "45 minutes after / before" (4 times); geo 1 perimeter (rectangle with two sides shown, polygons of 3-6 sides with every side labelled, sides 1-20); geo 2 area (count a grid to 6 x 8, or length x width with labels to 12 x 12, in square units); shape 5 quadrilaterals, "NOT a rhombus", "NOT a quadrilateral", right angles, sides and names of pentagon / hexagon / octagon; data 3 bar graph or picture graph at scale 2, 5 or 10 (half symbols at 2 and 10), read / compare / total; meas 4 ruler to the half or quarter inch (choice of 4 mixed numbers) and scale / jug reading (g, kg, mL, L); story 6 two steps, all four operations, answers to 909.
- Edits to existing files: `38-skills.js` (dispatchers for `time`, `shape`, `meas`, `sub`, `story`, `data`; new `round`, `geo`), `42-paths.js` (the `m_frac` node list), `src/css/32-frac.css` (`.choices.ffc`, `.ffrow`, tokens only), `tools/build.py` (VER v76, provisional), `tools/test.js`, `tools/smoke.js`, `tools/shots.js`. No new emoji (target and triangle ruler are on the allowlist), no new colours.

Math world count is unchanged (m_frac already existed), so no world-count expectation moved.
- Tests added: test.js 48 checks pass (46 + 2 new). "Fraction Forest P2-07: stops ... 3,000 samples per new level": 12 new levels + the 4 reserved ones, 3,000 seeded questions each, zero errors; the maths is recomputed from the markup (numbers read from the prompt, the clock pictures rebuilt and compared, regrouping counted digit by digit, the bar and picture graphs rebuilt from the label and compared, the dial needle and the jug water level read back as a value, the story numbers read from the spoken text) plus fair-share checks for every variant and all 60 minutes of time 5; this check found two real faults before release (sub 7 `X00 - b` with a zero ones digit regrouped only once, and time 5 clock choices one tick apart) and both are fixed. "Fraction Forest P2-07: stops added to m_frac keep every star ...": see Save safety. smoke.js: one new block (P2-07 at 380x780, midway fixture, grade 3): plays the 12 new stops in map order with real taps and the number pad, checks nothing is wider than the screen, 48 px keys and choices, a picture on the picture stops, stars saved, then 12 NEW tags on a finished forest. ### P4-10 Question screen, keypads, feedback (v75, 2026-11-11, Claude)
- What changed (DESIGN_SYSTEM 9 row 4): the round screen is rebuilt on the P4-04 components, styles in the new `src/css/42-question.css` (tokens only, transform/opacity motion). Top bar: round close button, a track with one dot per question (green done, amber "needed another try", ringed current) on a rail that fills as you go, the drawn pet walking from dot to dot to a gift (bolt for test-out) with its speech bubble, a flame streak chip (grey until 2 in a row) and a star chip; on phones the track gets its own row. Prompt on a white card with a subject-colour top edge (math blue, reading berry, Spanish green). "Hear it" and "Help" are soft icon buttons; Pre-K gets a big round blue replay button and a round amber bulb (no words). The hint is an amber panel with a bulb badge. Review tag and Challenge tag are pills.
- Kinds (49-kinds.js render, contract unchanged): number pad on `.keys`/`.key` (64 px keys, 72 on tall tablets, 56 on phones; backspace and check icons; the check key shows an arrow while more boxes follow; `.` and +/- as a full-width yellow row); choices on `.choice` (green with a tick badge when right, amber with a soft cross badge when wrong, never colour alone, no pink); multi with tick boxes and a green Check button; build and order on `.piece`/`.socket` (white tiles, a grey well where a used tile was, letter slots as tinted wells); match as choice cards where a found pair shares a colour and a shape mark (circle, square, diamond, bar, ring); tap targets as raised pieces with badges; spelling keyboard on `.key` (edge to edge on phones, 64 px tall where it fits). The flying star and the burst are drawn stars (hidden in calm mode and reduced motion). Emoji removed from round chrome and toasts (Boom, Zap, streak). Prompt enters with a fade and 8 px rise, shakes 6 px on a wrong try; calm mode and reduced motion stop all of it.

### P4-08 Home screen redesign (v74, 2026-11-11, Claude)
- Why: Home is the first thing a child sees and still looked dated (emoji tiles, flat yellow hero, emoji action row). Rebuilt to DESIGN_SYSTEM 9 row 2 with the P4-04 components, P4-02 icons and the P4-05 dragon; no behaviour change.
- Look: top bar with a drawn logo mark (star on brand), round sound and gear icon buttons. Hero card: the pet stands on a small drawn scene that follows the shop scene (meadow, beach, candy, rainbow, space, castle; `--ill-*` tokens), name, pet-growth gauge with "N more (star) until X grows", today's mission as 3 pips, stat chips (stars, coins, streak flame = the calendar button, game-time ticket when there is time). A hungry/dirty/bored pet shows a thought bubble with the apple or ball drawing or drawn bath bubbles. "Find my level" card with subject icons. A Today row of three tiles: Review (count badge), Fact Sprint, Mixed Practice (or a green "All caught up!" tile). Path cards are big subject-colour buttons with a drawn picture on a white badge (toy number blocks, open book with ribbon, "¡Hola!" speech bubbles), a star-yellow gauge with "9/86", the next stop, and a white play disc (trophy when done). Game Room card with a drawn controller (lock disc with no game time). Secondary actions as icon tiles: Speed Race, Pet Care (with a "!" badge), Pet Shop, Stickers. Version label in the footer. On a wide landscape screen (900 px+) Home is two columns (hero + Today | games + actions). No emoji left in Home's chrome.

### P4-03: Remaining UI icons (v73, 2026-11-11, Claude)
- What changed: 66 new icons in `src/js/15-icons.js` (106 in all) and a two-tone house style. Utility (29): info, warning, question, circle, check-circle, download, upload, trash, edit, mic, record, stop, undo, redo, rotate, flip, eye, timer, clock, chart, user, users, arrow-right/left/up/down, chevron-right/left, more. Chrome and rewards (20): rocket, balloon, party, globe, moon, hat, teddy, image, food, bath, ball, drop, car, nut, palette, blocks, medal, sparkle, review, thumbs-up. Skills (17): skill-count, skill-num, skill-shape, skill-frac, skill-abc, skill-story, skill-pattern, skill-array, skill-geo, scale, ruler, ear, doc, music, clap, pin, wave. Refined (same names): home, map, gift, hint, path-disc, target, calendar got a soft second tone.
- Style (DESIGN_SYSTEM 5.2a, header of 15-icons.js): icons stay crisp glyphs that read at 20 px, but most carry a confident second tone: `fill="var(--ic3,currentColor)" opacity=".4"` for back planes, containers and lesser parts (house walls, tray, clock face, far person, lens). It follows the icon colour on white, on a brand fill and on a tint; `--ic3` can recolour it. The primary layer alone must read (test-enforced); soft shapes are areas 4+ units across, drawn first; symbols (check, close, plus, arrows, chevrons, star, heart, bolt) stay one tone. Same grid (24, artwork 2..22), round joins and caps, strokes 2 to 3.6 (arrows and chevrons 3 to 3.4), corners r 1.5 to 2.8, under 600 bytes each, one-decimal coordinates; no gradients, filters or text.

### P4-11 Rewards: finish, chest, stars, bosses (v72, 2026-11-11, Claude)
- What changed (DESIGN_SYSTEM 6.3, 8.1, 9 row 5): the round-complete moment is drawn instead of emoji. Before the tap: a big shaded treasure chest (3/4 view, barrel lid, gold bands, star stud, lock) on soft light rays, wiggling until tapped, with a "Tap the treasure chest!" pill. After the tap: a hero card with three reward stars (the smiling object-art star, earned ones pop in 120 ms apart, empty slots grey), the score ("10 out of 10") and the warm line; a gold "treasure" strip with the open chest (gold coins, gems, sparkles) and the stickers popping out as die-cut white stickers with a slight turn and a "New" tag; message cards with an icon disc (next stop unlocked, world finished, boss beaten with the drawn dizzy boss, new costume in a prize rosette, daily mission, streak, practice); an "earned" list (+stars counting up, +game time with every reason on its own line with an icon, the hint line, +coins); a dock that stays at the bottom with one primary next step (Next stop, or Play again / Try again), Bonus game and Map / Home. Wide landscape puts hero and treasure on the left and the list above the dock on the right. Drawn confetti (about 24 token-coloured pieces, 1.1 s) bursts from the chest and from the world-finished card; calm mode and reduced motion hide it.
- Kind low score: no empty-star row; the child's pet cheers in the hero (drawn dragon with its costume, emoji for other pets), "Try again" is the primary button and the "Get 7 right to open the next stop. You can do it!" line sits right under the score as a tip (the test-out "So close!" line too).
- Bosses: Gloop (purple jelly, one tooth, waving arm), Robo-Bonk (aqua robot, screen face, red antenna ball, springy arms), Captain Inky (pink octopus, pirate hat, curly moustache), Zorp (green, three eyes, antennae), each with an idle face and a dizzy hit face (squeezed eyes, "oh" mouth, ring of stars) in one SVG. Boss intro: the boss on a spotlight (bobs three times), seven zap bolts, the prize in a rosette, "Let's go!" in the subject colour. In the round's top bar `G.boss.e` is now the drawing; a zap (`.bossE.hit`) shows the hit face, and the old `hit` keyframes (which used a filter) are replaced by a transform-only one. Test out and the pet-growth overlay (#yay) are now cards over a dim backdrop (bolt disc; pet on light rays, primary "Yay!").
- No behaviour change: stars, coins, stickers, prizes, mission, streak reward, game time and saved data are computed by the same untouched lines of finish(); the sequence (sticker every 600 ms, a coin sound per star, buttons, a message every 650 ms) and its sounds are the same (the closing "superstar / keep practicing" line moved from the last message to the hero, so the sequence is 650 ms shorter). Smoke checks the deltas.
- Selectors kept: `#fin`, `#chest` (now a drawn button with aria-label "Open the treasure chest"), `#btns` (hidden until the star count ends, then visible), `#bonus`, `#nxt`, `#again`, `#menu`, `#prz`, `#msgs`, `#sc`, `#yay`, `.ovl .grow .pet-svg`, `.ovl .grown .pet-svg`, `#fight`, `#nope`, `#tgo`, `#tno`, `.bossE`, `#bossE`, `.hit`, `.ko`. ### P2-04 2nd grade gaps: world Treasure Bay (v70, 2026-11-11, Claude)
- What changed: new math world `m_bay` (Treasure Bay, grade 2) between Space Station and Multiply Mountain (CURRICULUM 2.1 #8) with exactly the M-2 stops in order: `pv 2, big 5, pv 3, sub 5, time 4, coins 4, meas 3, data 2, arr 1, cnt 5, coins 5, story 4, pat 3` plus the boss. New generators in one block at the end of `src/js/31-gen-math-early.js` (headed `// ---- P2-04 Treasure Bay (2nd) ----`, helper object `BAY`, functions `genBayPv2`, `genBayPv3`, `genBayBig5`, `genBaySub5`, `genBayTime4`, `genBayCoins4`, `genBayCoins5`, `genBayMeas3`, `genBayData2`, `genBayArr1`, `genBayCnt5`, `genBayStory4`, `genBayPat3`). Every level has a hint (multi-step ones are a numbered `hsteps` list, shown all at once until P2-08 adds the stepper), `say`, a unique label under 60 characters, no new question kinds.
- Edits to existing files: `38-skills.js` (each extended skill got a level dispatcher `lv==N?genBayX():oldGen(lv)`; `max` raised: pv 1->3, big 4->5, sub 4->5, time 3->4, coins 3->5, meas 2->3, data 1->2, cnt 4->5, pat 2->3; `arr` and `story` already had `max` 2 and 5, their reserved levels `arr 1` and `story 4` now ask the new questions), `42-paths.js` (one world line), `tools/build.py` (VER), tests. Old levels give byte-identical questions for the same seed (test compares 60 seeds of every old level against the old generator).
- Picture notes: `VIZ.ruler` limits a cm ruler to 15 (CURRICULUM says 0-20), so cm rulers run 0-15 and inch rulers 0-12. Coins 5 pictures use `picFor` for the item. Money amounts are written `$3.45`.
- Tests changed (old -> new): `RESERVED` in "skills: every level is reachable" `{arr:[1],story:[4],frac:[6,7,8]}` -> `{frac:[6,7,8]}`. No other expectation changed: every world-count expectation (placement, mastery, smoke placement run) is computed from PATHS/NODES/PLACE and passed unchanged. New: test.js "Treasure Bay (P2-04)" (world position and stop ids, old levels unchanged, 3,000 samples per new level with answers by rule, label diversity, seeded reproducibility, grade gating, saves compared with a path without the world for 6 fixtures x 6 grades = 3,096 stop checks, harder/easier follow map order); smoke.js `// P2-04` block (map position, first round, all 13 stops answered by taps at 380x780); shots.js group `bay-*` and helper `bayShot`.
- Save safety: stars are keyed by stop id (the 13 stop ids plus `boss_m_bay` are new keys; no existing key changes meaning). Proven by fixtures (fresh, midway, maxed, tricky, both legacy maps) at grades none/0/1/2/3/6: every stop keeps its stars with and without the world, no passed stop re-locks, nothing open is locked except an untouched world above the child's grade (the "inserted world" exemption, not weakened), no new stop starts with stars, loading changes no map. The map opens on the same stop as before in 32 of 36 loads; in the other 4 the child had finished Space Station and not touched Treasure Bay, so they now meet it next (asserted). Nobody with progress beyond it, or at grade 3 and up, is sent back into it (`curNode` untouched).

### TOOL-01: Faster smoke (no app change) (2026-11-11, Claude)
- Why: a full smoke run took 10-20 minutes and a run crossing midnight broke the Daily Review counts.
- `tools/lib/browser.js` injects a virtual clock (context.addInitScript, before app code): `Date` starts at 2026-06-15 10:00 local and runs at SMOKE_SPEED (default 8) times real time; `setTimeout` / `setInterval` delays and `performance.now` are scaled the same; `requestAnimationFrame` and CSS animations are untouched. `SMOKE_SPEED=1` = the real clock (no faking at all). shots.js uses the same helper (fixed day; screenshots checked).
- Fixtures only use the day key "2000-1-1", so none needed changes. smoke.js tweaks (all marked TOOL-01): `vwait(page, ms)` (virtual-time wait) for the 8 waits that wait on app timers; an extra `vwait 2800` before the passage step (a stale next() from the number-pad reveal fires mid-round otherwise); the map-draw timing uses the real clock (`window.__realNow`). No step skipped or loosened.
- Wall clock (run alone, machine busy with other runs): smoke 574 s before (SMOKE_SPEED=1), 230 s after; check.sh about 580 s before, 236 s after. Speed 20 was faster (197 s) but broke the reveal steps, so 8 stays.
- Tests: check.sh ok, smoke passed 3 runs in a row at speed 8.

### P4-13b: Object art set A redrawn, new illustration style (v69, 2026-11-11, Claude)
- Why: the owner found the P4-13 drawings flat and dated ("1999 graphics"); the cause was mostly the old 6.5 rule (flat two-tone, under 900 bytes, no shading). This chunk resets the style and redraws all 40 set A pictures with the same names (`picFor`/`picArt`/`ART` unchanged).
- New style (DESIGN_SYSTEM 6.5 rewritten): soft chunky shapes with real volume; up to four tones per colour area from one hue (base, shade lower right, highlight upper left, deep accent for creases and insides); light always from the top left; no outlines except a deep-tone edge on white or cream objects; faces only on animals, the sun and the star; 3/4 view for boxy things; every picture must read as THE word at 24 px, 48 px and 1em. Gradients are not used (repeated pictures on one screen would repeat ids); volume comes from layered tonal shapes and a few opacity glints.
- Palette: new `--ill-*` tokens in `src/css/00-tokens.css` (red, orange, yellow, green, aqua, blue, purple, pink, brown, tan, skin, cream, grey x 4 tones, plus white x 3 and ink x 2). `src/css/21-art.css` maps the `--a-*` roles to them (x1 highlight, x base, x2 shade, x3 deep; new hues c aqua, s skin, m cream, e grey, k ink). `.art` fill defaults to ink.
- The 8 weak pictures: nose is now a skin-tone front-view nose with bridge, tip highlight, nostril wings and dark nostrils (was a yellow blob); box is an open cardboard box in 3/4 view with flaps, dark inside and tape (was toast); map is a three-panel folded paper map with a river, land, trees, a dotted path and a red pin (was a green sheet with an X); rose is a side-view bloom of cupped, layered petals with sepals, thorny stem and leaves (was a lollipop spiral); bed is a chunky side-view wooden bed with tall rounded headboard, pillow, turned-back sheet and blanket; hand is an open palm with tapered fingers, nails, knuckle creases, a thumb and a blue sleeve cuff (the sleeve and nails stop it reading as a glove); hat is a straw sun hat with a red band and bow (was a purple top hat); milk is a glass of milk with a shaded surface and glass rim (was a carton).
- Other changes for "no other name": cat is now grey with whiskers (the orange fox face is the only orange pointy-eared face); bug is a green beetle (not a ladybug); cup sits on a saucer (not a mug); ship has funnels, decks and portholes (not a boat); the beach ball pole cap was dropped.
- Size: `16-art-a.js` 18.3 KB -> 59.3 KB source (pictures 52.7 KB, average 1.3 KB, largest hand 2.4 KB); index.html 665 KB -> 709 KB (+43.5 KB), well under the 1.2 MB warning. No filters, masks or gradients; at most 30 elements per picture.
- Tests: test.js "art set A" updated to the new rules: 40 present, at most 2,500 bytes and 40 elements each, set under 64 KB, only path/circle/ellipse/rect self-closing, only geometry/fill/stroke/stroke-width/opacity/rotate attributes, no ids/defs/gradients/masks/clips/patterns/filters/use/text/style/url/raw hex, colours only var(--a-x)/none/currentColor, one-decimal coordinates, well-formed picArt, every role used is defined, every role in 21-art.css maps to an `--ill-*` hex token, picture questions show drawings. Checked that it fails on an injected id/style/two-decimal coordinate and on a role mapped to a non-ill token. "pictures" check unchanged and passing.
- Screens: `node tools/shots.js art art-question`; looked at the art sheet at 1024x600 (24/48/96 px on white and tint) and art-question at 380x780, plus many scratch sheets at 160/96/48/24 px and 1em in text during the passes.
- Edits: `src/js/16-art-a.js` (rewritten), `src/css/21-art.css`, `src/css/00-tokens.css` (new token block only), `src/js/15-pics.js` (header comment only), `docs/DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` 6.5, `tools/test.js`, `tools/build.py` VER.

### P4-04 Core components (v68, 2026-11-11, Claude)
- What changed: the component library of DESIGN_SYSTEM 4, all states, as opt-in classes in the new `src/css/10-components.css` (tokens only) and markup helpers `UI.*` in the new `src/js/14-ui.js`. Buttons (`.btn` + `primary` / `soft` / `subject` / `ghost` / `risk`, sizes `sm` / md / `lg`, `wide`, `.btn.icon` round 48/56/64), keys (`.keys`, `.key`, `.go`, `.del`, letter pad), choices (`.choice` + `pic` / `sym` / `wide` in `.picks`; `.sel`, `.right` with a CSS check badge and pop, `.wrong` with an amber cross badge and shake, disabled), pieces and sockets, panels (plain, tint, hero), path cards, top bars (`.top.tbar`, `.round`, `.adult`), stat chips (`.stat` star / coin / ticket / flame / timer, bump), gauge, round dots, star rating, tags, toast, speech bubble, sheet (modal), hint panel (text, picture, steps), context panel, segmented control, switch, table, empty state, map node. Subject colour by class `.s-math` ... `.s-play`. Look (owner feedback, "no dated flat look"): solid ledge under everything pressable that squashes on press (transform only), thin gloss on coloured fills, soft blur shadow only on panels, sheets and toasts, generous corners.
- Names: classes the app already uses with another look are not touched; those components got new names (DS 4 table updated): card -> `.panel`, chip -> `.stat`, meter -> `.gauge`, tile/slot -> `.piece`/`.socket`, bubble -> `.speech`, node -> `.mapnode`, empty -> `.blank`, danger button -> `.btn.risk`. A bare `.btn` and `.top` keep their old look. test.js fails if 10-components.css restyles an old class.
- Live app: nothing changes. No existing screen uses the new classes yet (P4-08, P4-10, P4-11, P4-12, P4-16 switch their screens). Before/after home, question, grown at all three sizes: grown identical pixel for pixel; home and question differ only where they differ between two runs of the same build (pet bob, random question).
- Edits to existing files: `00-tokens.css` (one block of component tokens: `--ledge-soft`, `--danger-shade`, `--play-deep`, `--hi`, `--hi-0`, `--dim`, `--drop`), `tools/contrast.js` (45 new pairs, 87 in all), `tools/test.js` (new check "components (P4-04)"), `tools/shots.js` (P4-04 group: `components`, `comp-sheet`, `comp-toast`; helpers `compSheet`, `compCheck`, `compLive` after SCREENS), `tools/build.py` (VER), DESIGN_SYSTEM 4 (rewritten to the shipped API), CONTRIBUTING 3.2 (file row for 14-ui.js). No smoke change (no live screen changed).
- Tests: test.js 37 of 37 (new check: no hex or colour functions, no filter, transitions and keyframes only transform/opacity, k-* keyframe names unique, every var() is a token, no old class or bare .btn/.top restyled, 40+ helper assertions incl. escaping, meter clamping, aria labels, stars, dots, steps; proved to fail on an injected `.card` rule, a background transition, a width keyframe, an unknown token and an unescaped label). Contrast 87 pairs pass. Sheet self-check (`node tools/shots.js components comp-sheet comp-toast`): no overflow, no word broken over two lines, every target at least 48 px, no sideways scroll at 600x960, 1024x600, 380x780 (it caught and fixed: "Grown-/ups" breaking at the hyphen, a 7-key letter row too wide for 380, round dots overlapping a phone top bar, a toast wrapping to 3 lines). check.sh OK (build, contrast, hex guard, test.js, smoke.js with no console errors and no network). index.html 706 KB (+41 KB: CSS 30 KB, JS 11 KB).

### P4-05 Pet rig and dragon, 4 stages, 6 faces (v67, 2026-11-11, Claude)
- What changed: new `src/js/17-pets-svg.js` (the rig) and `src/css/41-pet.css` (palette roles and motion). The dragon is now a drawn, shaded character instead of the emoji on Home, Pet Shop, Pet Care, the map, the round runner, the growth overlay and the setup choice. Other pets (dino, bird, sea pal) are exactly as before until P4-06.
- Rig API (for P4-06 / P4-07): `petSVG(kind, stage, mood, {hat, toy, react, super, cls})` returns one inline SVG (viewBox 0 0 200 200, ground y 184) built from groups `pg-shadow, pg-glow (super), pg-fig > pg-extra (pg-xl, pg-xr), pg-tail, pg-body, pg-belly, pg-arm-l, pg-arm-r (holds the toy), pg-head (holds the hat) > pg-cheeks, pf > pg-eyes, pg-mouth`. Stages `egg, baby, kid, grown` (or the `stage()` number 0-4; 4 = grown + glow ring). Moods `idle, happy, oops, cheer, sleepy, hungry` swap only eyes and mouth (`PET_FACE`). `PET_RIG[kind] = {stages: {egg|baby|kid|grown: proportions + anchors}, egg, wing, tail, arm, body(P), belly(P), head(P), cheeks}`; shading helpers `petHi`, `petBlob`. Anchors `hat` / `toy` = `[x, y, scale, degrees]` per stage (`petAnchor(kind, stage, 'hat'|'toy')`): a hat's bottom centre sits on the hat anchor and turns with the head; the toy's centre sits on the toy anchor in the right arm group. SVG wearables (P4-07) are 64x64-grid markup: hats attach at (32, 60), toys at (32, 32); emoji wearables (today's `S.wear`) are drawn as SVG text at the same anchors. Hooks: `petRig(kind)`, `petMood(care)`, `petRigHTML(st)`, `petArt(st)`, `petPick(kind)`.
- Moods from existing state (`petMood`, Pet Care meters): food < 30 hungry, clean or fun < 30 sleepy, all 80+ happy, else idle. `react: true` adds the cheer and oops faces, shown by CSS while `anim()` puts `.jump` / `.sad` on the container: tapping the pet, a snack or a right answer show cheer, a wrong answer or the bath show oops. Motion is CSS on groups, transform/opacity only: idle bob with its shadow, blink every 4 s, egg rocks, happy wags the tail, cheer hops and flaps, oops wobbles, sleepy breathes slowly with a tilted head; reduced motion and `html.calm` stop them (global rule in 20-base.css).
- Style: after owner feedback (see Ideas and issues) the dragon has 3-4 tones per area (shade rim, base, white highlight, dark accent), light from the top left, chunky proportions, big eyes with two glints. Palette roles `--pc-*` are built from tokens in 41-pet.css (`.pet-k-dragon`), like 21-art.css. No filters, gradients, ids or SMIL.
- Edits to existing files (exact): `05-pets.js` `petAura` returns `'rig'` for a rig pet (else unchanged); `42-paths.js` `petHTML` starts with `if(petRig())return petRigHTML(st);`; `46-home.js` setup pet choice `${p.s[3]}` -> `${petPick(k)}`; `50-round.js` growShow `${petE(before)}` -> `${petArt(before)}`; `20-base.css` `.petbig` and `.pet.aura,.petbig.aura` use `text-shadow` instead of `filter: drop-shadow` (same look for emoji pets; closes the P4-01 note); `tools/build.py` VER.
- Saved data: nothing new is stored and nothing changes meaning (`S.pet`, `S.stars` -> `stage()`, `S.wear`, `S.care` are only read; `careTick()` is the existing call). A midway child (dragon, 85 stars) gets the kid dragon with its top hat and balloon; a dino child sees exactly today's markup (test compares the old string).
- Size: 17-pets-svg.js 16.3 KB (dragon part 6.3 KB, the rest is the rig, faces and the doc header), 41-pet.css 3.7 KB; index.html 665,216 -> 685,247 bytes (+20 KB). One rendered pet is 7-9 KB of markup (about 70 shapes).
- Tests: test.js new check "pet rig (P4-05)" (5 stages x 6 moods well formed, named groups, one face per mood and only the face changes, reaction faces, wearables in the head / right-arm groups, SVG wearable attach points, anchors, tokens only, palette roles defined, deterministic, budget, petMood mapping, midway save untouched, other pets identical to today, 41-pet.css animates only transform/opacity, no filter left in 20-base.css). smoke.js block `// P4-05` (380x780 midway: home pet class, hat, toy, no filter, no overflow; tap -> cheer face; Pet Care hungry -> idle after a snack, bath -> oops face; map pet; runner cheer after a right answer; growth overlay egg -> kid; super; reduced motion and calm; dino unchanged; setup shows the drawn dragon). shots.js group `pets` (sheet: every stage x mood, anchors with their 64-grid boxes, emoji wearables, reactions, sizes, dark scene), `pets-home`, `pets-care`.
- Screenshots: before in tools/out/before/ (home, care, shop, map, question at all three sizes); after: pets (all sizes), pets-home, pets-care at all three sizes. Looked at: pets sheet 1024x600, pets-home 380x780 and 600x960, pets-care 380x780, plus scratch close-ups at real size while restyling. Map, runner and overlay checked by measured geometry (no overflow, map pet beside its stop).

### P2-03 1st grade gaps: world Clock Tower (v66, 2026-11-11, Claude)
- What changed: new math world `m_clock` (Clock Tower, grade 1) between Candy Canyon and Ocean Reef (CURRICULUM 2.1 #5) with exactly the M-1 stops in order: `pv 1, add 5, meas 2, add 6, data 1, shape 4, frac 1` plus the boss. New generators in one block at the end of `src/js/31-gen-math-early.js` (headed `// ---- P2-03 Clock Tower (1st) ----`, helper object `CT`): `genPv1` (base-ten picture / "how many tens in 47" / `30 + 6`, mixed evenly), `genAdd5` (three addends, sum <= 20, 60% have a pair making ten or a double, the rest neither), `genAdd6` (+10, -10, + multiple of 10, +/- inside the same ten), `genMeas` (pencil or paper strip above 1-12 unit cubes), `genData1` (picture graph, 3 categories, values 1-8 all different, count / most / least / how many more / total of two), `genShape` (level 4: sides, corners, "which shape has 4 sides"; 6 shapes, corner dots and flashing sides in the hints), `genFrac1` (circles and rectangles cut in 2 or 4 equal or unequal parts: halves, fourths, "is it cut into equal parts?"). Every level has a hint, every question has `say`.
- Clock picture: `genTime` now draws `VIZ.clock(h, m)`; `clockSVG` (raw hex) is deleted. Answers, choices and labels are unchanged (tested against the picture). The two hint sentences say "short pink hand" instead of "short red hand" (the token colour of the hour hand is pink-red). Before/after screenshots of time levels 1 and 3 looked right.
- Edits to existing files: `38-skills.js` (new skills `pv`, `meas`, `data`, `frac` as one contiguous group after `mbig`; `add` max 3 -> 6 with a dispatcher, `shape` max 1 -> 4 with `genShape`), `42-paths.js` (one world inserted after `m_candy`), `32-gen-read.js` (genTime, clockSVG removed), `51-mastery.js` (`mastNear` by level, see Ideas and issues), tools only otherwise.
- Tests changed (old -> new): test.js "skills: every level is reachable" `RESERVED` `{arr:[1],story:[4]}` -> adds `add:[4],shape:[2,3],meas:[1]` (P2-02 levels); placement "start world per grade on math" uses world ids instead of indexes 0,1,2,4; placement "all right on math" expects `hi=min(nw-1, PLACE.MAX)`: 7 worlds now end on the last world after 5 testlets with `all` false, skip = worlds 0..5 (was `all` true with 6 worlds); placement simulated "allRight" accepts "every world, or five testlets in a row"; mastery harder/easier invariant path order -> level order; Tiny Town skill max check `!==` -> `<` (later chunks raise the max). smoke.js: no old expectation changed (the placement step computes from the world count and still ends on the last world after 20 questions). New: test.js "Clock Tower (P2-03)" (3,000 samples per level, answers by rule, label/answer, grade gating, saves compared with a path without the world for 5 fixtures x 6 grades = 1740 stop checks, mastery chain add 1,2,3,5,6); smoke.js `// P2-03` block (map position, first round, all stops answered by taps at 380x780, time stop draws the VIZ clock); shots.js group `clock-*` and helper `clockShot`.
- Save safety: stars are keyed by stop id (`pv1`, `add5`, `meas2`, `add6`, `data1`, `shape4`, `frac1`, `boss_m_clock` are new keys; `time1` etc. keep their meaning). Proven by fixtures (midway, maxed, fresh, both legacy maps) at grades none/0/1/2/3/6: every stop has the same stars with and without the world, no passed stop re-locks, nothing open is locked except an untouched world above the child's grade (the "inserted world" exemption of the worlds check, which is not weakened), no new stop starts with stars, loading changes no map. A child who had finished Candy Canyon and not started Ocean Reef (K grade) now meets Clock Tower first; grade 1 and up see both open.
- Tests: test.js 37 checks pass; smoke.js passes; contrast and hex guard pass.
- Screenshots looked at (380x780 unless noted): clock-map, clock-pv1a/b, clock-meas2, clock-data1a/b, clock-shape4a/b, clock-frac1a/b, clock-time3, clock-hint-meas2, clock-hint-shape4, time1 before/after (600x960). All 17 entries exist at the three sizes.

### P2-02 K math: world Shape Garden (v65, 2026-11-11, Claude)
- What changed: new math world `m_shape` "Shape Garden" (g 0, K), the second math world, between Tiny Town and Number Meadow, with exactly the CURRICULUM M-K stops in order: pk_count 3, pk_num 3, tenf 1, pk_more 2, shape 2, add 4, pat 2, tenf 2, cnt 4, shape 3, sort 3, pos 1, meas 1, tenf 3 (14 stops plus the boss `boss_m_shape`). New skills `tenf` (3 levels), `pos` (1), `meas` (1); levels added to `pk_count` (3), `pk_num` (3), `pk_more` (2), `shape` (3), `add` (4), `pat` (2), `cnt` (4), `sort` (3). Existing levels are untouched: `38-skills.js` points those skills at small wrappers (`genPkCountK` etc.) that call the old generator for the old levels.
- Generators: one block at the end of `src/js/31-gen-math-early.js` headed `// ---- P2-02 Shape Garden (K) ----` (no Math.random). Ten frames of objects in rows of 5 for 11-20 (`sgFrames`), teens by ear with reversed and "ty" look-alikes, more / fewer / same plates up to 10 (same in 15% of items: 2/3 equal, 1/3 off by one; yes / no with the check and close icons), hexagon / rhombus / trapezoid / oval turned and stretched with `VIZ.shape`, solids by name and by real thing (ball, can, box, ice cream cone, drum, dice), +0 sums within 5 with `VIZ.dots`, count on or back by 1 (40% back), AAB / ABB / ABC patterns of 6-7, sort by kind (animals, food, vehicles, clothes), `VIZ.tenFrame` stops (how many, how many more to make ten, ten and some more, forward and reverse), position scenes (above, below, beside, between, in front of, behind), measuring (crayons that may not line up, towers, a tipped scale). Same audio-first presentation as Tiny Town (`pkQ`, `pkTxt`, `q.pk`: spoken on its own, big replay button, words only in `.pktxt`), so no new presentation and no new question kinds (`num`, `choice`, `tap`). Every level has a spoken picture hint, `say`, a unique label under 60 characters, and every picture of a thing goes through `picFor`.
- New file `src/css/31-sg.css` (tokens only). Edits to existing files: `38-skills.js` (8 entries repointed, one contiguous group of 3 new skills), `42-paths.js` (the world; `curNode` below), `tools/build.py` (VER).
- `curNode()` (42-paths.js, one line): an untouched world that lies behind the child's furthest progress is skipped, like an untouched world below the start world already was. Without it a K child who finished Number Meadow (or midway, maxed, tricky) would be sent back to Count to 20 because the start world for K is now Shape Garden. A child with no stars at all opens on pk_count3 (K) as before for Pre-K (Tiny Town) and from grade 1 (their world).
- Save safety: stars are keyed by stop id (`pk_count3`, `boss_m_shape`, never an index), `MAP_V42` is untouched. New test "Shape Garden (P2-02): world position, stop ids, no star moves, nothing re-locks": every fixture (fresh, midway, maxed, tricky, both legacy) at every grade (none, Pre-K to 6th) is loaded with and without the new world; every existing stop keeps its stars, nothing that was open is locked, no passed stop is locked, the saved map is not changed by loading, new stops carry no stars, the map opens on the same stop as before (except a child with no stars at all, who opens on Count to 20 at K), and the map never opens in Shape Garden for anyone with progress or at any grade but K. Proven to fail when the `curNode` line is removed.
- Test and smoke expectations changed (old -> new, all computed from PATHS / NODES / PLACE): placement "start world per grade on math" literals (1, 2, 4) -> world ids (m_shape, m_candy, m_space); "all right on math": the quiz is 5 testlets at most, so a 1st grader starting on the first K world passes 5 of 7 worlds (placed on the 6th, `all` false) instead of every world; the simulated fixtures expect `all` only if first world + 5 testlets reaches the end; mastery "easier(add1) is null" -> the add stop just before it on the map (add 4); Tiny Town test: skill `max` equal -> at least (later chunks add levels), "K opens on Number Meadow" -> Shape Garden; worlds test: a child with no stars may open on the first stop of the start world; smoke: the number pad step opens the Number Meadow chip first, the midway map and "more worlds" step use world ids, the placement run expects the map on the 6th world. The pat label was shortened to stay under 60 characters (new level only).

The first one checks per level: audio-first, hints, no text outside `.pktxt`, counted objects equal the answer, frames, look-alike numerals, plate counts and the 15% "same", polygon corners of each drawn shape, solids by thing, dots, count-by-1 sequences, pattern continuation, categories, tap order of the scenes, bar widths, tower heights, scale answer, seeded reproducibility, no raw hex, no Math.random. Smoke: new block `// P2-02` (a K child opens on Shape Garden; a full 8-question round by taps with every prompt spoken and the big replay button; then each of the 14 stops is answered once with all text hidden: no letters in the prompt, targets 48 px or more, no sideways scroll, a hint on each; the map shows 14 stops and the boss). Contrast and hex guard pass; size 691,006 bytes.

### P2-06 Fractions core (v63, 2026-11-11, Claude)
- What changed: new skill `frac` (levels 2-8, `max: 8`) with generators in one block at the end of `src/js/33-gen-math-mid.js` (headed `// ---- P2-06 fractions core ----`, entry `genFracCore`): 2 name the shaded fraction (bar or circle, number pad `frac`) or pick the picture for a named fraction; 3 tap the point on a 0-1 line (d 2-5) or read the marked point (0-1, sometimes 0-2, `eq:'value'`); 4 compare with bars, same bottom or same top, `< = >`; 5 `1/2 = ?/8` (bars half the time) or "which equals 2/3" (3 choices, no distractor equal in value); 6 compare unlike fractions, no picture (benchmark 1/2 or common bottom hint, 15% equal pairs); 7 simplest form (GCF 2-6, d <= 48, exact); 8 `2 3/4 = ?/4` and `11/4 =` whole and fraction (`sep:'mix'`). Every level has a hint, `say` (fractions spoken "3 fourths") and a unique label; seeded randomness only. New `src/css/32-frac.css` (`.fr` stacked fraction span, tokens only).
- Stops: new math world `m_frac` Fraction Forest (grade 3, appended after Multiply Mountain, CURRICULUM 2.1 #10) with `frac 2, frac 3, frac 4, frac 5` plus the boss (5 map stops). The world did not exist yet, so it was created here in CURRICULUM order; P2-07 inserts the rest (round, sub 6, geo, ...) between these stops in the 2.1 order and must keep these four ids. frac 1 is P2-03's: until it ships `genFracCore(1)` asks like level 2 (the merge should wrap `frac.gen` as `lv => lv == 1 ? genFrac1(lv) : genFracCore(lv)` and register `frac` once). ### P5-03 Daily Review and Tricky Mix on the new engine (v56, 2026-11-10, Claude)
- What changed: Home has a "Today's review" card under the hero: with items due it reads "N due" (N = `dueItems().length`, the whole backlog) and a Start button; with none it is a kind green "All caught up! Nothing to review today." with no button. Start runs `startReview()`: a round (mix-style: no stars, no map progress) holding the 10 most urgent due items (`dueItems(10)` rebuilt with `revQ`) and nothing else, so with 12 due the round asks those first, most urgent first; with 3 due it is a 3-question round. With nothing due `startReview()` falls back to Mixed Practice, so an empty round is never built. Play again on the finish screen starts the next review while items remain. Answers book through the existing `check()` hooks (right = up a box and counted as fixed for game time, wrong = box 0 again).
- Tricky Mix is replaced by the card: the home button in the action row is now always "Mixed Practice" (`#mix`, `startMix()` unchanged: up to 7 due items then stops the child has reached, never a boss stop; the boss-stop fix from v55 is kept and still tested).
- New: `src/css/34-review.css` (tokens only, `rv-` classes); `reviewQs`, `startReview`, `revCard`, `revCardBind` appended to `src/js/59-review.js`. Review engine rules are untouched.
- Edits to existing files: `46-home.js` three small edits (`${revCard()}` after `${placeBanner()}`; the `#mix` label is the fixed text "Mixed Practice" instead of the Tricky Mix (N) ternary; `revCardBind();` after `placeBannerBind();`), `50-round.js` one edit (Play again: `g.mix?(g.daily?startReview():startMix()):...`), `tools/build.py` VER.
- Tests: test.js "daily review" (tricky fixture with 12 due: card says 12, round holds the 10 most urgent in `dueItems` order and `makeQ` hands them out in that order; 3 due = 3 questions; a right answer books the item; nothing due = kind card, no button, `startReview` calls Mixed Practice and builds no round; future items are not due; card and questions build on every fixture). smoke.js `// P5-03` (tricky fixture at 380x780: card shows 21, Start 48 px or more, no horizontal overflow; round of 10 rebuilt due items, all right, 10 fixed, home says 11 due; with `S.rev=[]` the card is the empty state and `startReview()` still plays a full round). The P5-02 smoke check now expects "Mixed Practice" on the button. shots.js: review-due, review-none.

### FIX: Mixed Practice / Tricky Mix crash after a boss is beaten (in v55, 2026-11-10, Claude)
- Cause: `startMix` filled the round from every reached stop, including boss stops, which are not skills (`SK.boss` is undefined), so a random pick of a boss stop threw and the child stayed on Home. Pre-existing since v30; found when the P5-02 smoke step started failing at random after merging.
- Fix: the pool skips boss stops and unknown skills; picking moved into `mixQs()` so it can be tested. Merged in the same commit as P5-02 (v55).
- Test: new named check "mix" builds 300 mixes on every fixture; proved it fails without the fix (midway, maxed, tricky).

### P5-02 Spaced review engine (v55, 2026-11-10, Claude)
- What changed: new `src/js/59-review.js`. Leitner boxes over missed questions in `S.rev = [{sk, lv, seed, label, box, due, n, bk?}]` (max 150; `bk` only for bank questions). A first wrong try (`addMiss`) puts the fact in box 0, due today; a right first try while it is due moves it up a box, due 0, 1, 3, 7, 21 days later (day numbers from `mastDay()` = `STRK.idx(today())`); right in box 4 = it leaves; wrong at any time = box 0 again. One item per fact (key `sk|lv|label`); over the cap the highest box goes first (then least missed, then oldest). Items are rebuilt, never stored as html: `bankRebuild(bk)` for bank questions, else `genQ(sk, lv, seed)`; a different label (generator changed) gives a fresh question of the same skill and level and keeps the item. Challenge misses (P1-08, harder level) do not become items.
- In rounds: `makeQ` asks `revSlot()` after `mastQ()`: in a normal stop round, questions 4 and 8 take the most urgent due item of the path (else the old "older stop" review), questions 2 and 6 a due item of the stop itself; tag "Remember this one?"; once per item per round. This replaces the old `Math.random()<.3` stored-miss copy. Nothing due = the v53 round exactly (the smoke bot's paths are unchanged). Tricky Mix is built from `allMiss()` = due items rebuilt with `revQ`; a right answer to a due item counts as "fixed" (game time) as before.
- API for P5-03: `dueItems(n, path, d)` (most urgent first; `dueItems().length` = due today), `revQ(it)` (question with `q.rvk`, booked to the item when answered), `revMiss(q, d)`, `revRight(q, d)`, `revDue(it, d)`, `REV.get()`, `REV.INT`, `REV.CAP`. Header of 59-review.js has the full contract.
- Migration 5 (LATEST 5 -> 6, 00-core.js, self-contained, idempotent): every `P(sk).miss` entry holding a whole question with n > 0 becomes an item (box 0, due 0 = due now, n kept, seed and bk kept when present); each entry shrinks to the tally `{tot, q: {label}}` that the Grown-ups table reads; no `n` is left, so an older cached app finds nothing to replay. Stars, coins, pet, maps and everything else untouched.
- Hooks in existing files: `00-core.js` LATEST 6 + MIGRATIONS[5]; `42-paths.js` `allMiss` is now `const allMiss=()=>dueItems();`; `50-round.js` startMix maps `allMiss().slice(0,7)` through `revQ`, makeQ calls `revSlot()` and drops the 30% copy, addMiss keeps the tally and calls `revMiss(q)`, check() books `revRight(q)` on a right first try (replaces the `p.miss[...].n` decrement).
- Tests: test.js check "review" (new fixture `tools/fixtures/tricky.json`: a v53 save, schema 5, with 21 tricky misses stored as whole questions, 3 of them without seeds: 17,532 -> 5,085 bytes, 21 items, same Tricky count on home, no html, tally intact, stars/coins/pet/maps/mast/streak unchanged, idempotent, the Grown-ups cell and the v53 readers still work; all fixtures; bank spec kept; damaged entries skipped; migration cap keeps the 150 most missed; exact rebuild of 284 questions over every skill and level plus bank and passage under live bank memory; label-mismatch fallback; one item due on days 0, 1, 4, 11, 32 then gone; reset; clock set back; damaged S.rev; challenge misses; a 60-day calendar against an independent model of the rules (59 facts, 415 reviews, 77 wrong, 17 learned); cap with 250 extra misses drops the highest box every time; worst case 150 items = 31,351 bytes; round slots 2/4/6/8, once each, never in boss/test/mix/placement; Tricky Mix booking). Existing checks: the LATEST == 5 asserts in "grade" and "mastery" now read LATEST / MIGRATIONS.length. smoke.js block "P5-02" (tricky fixture migrates, home Tricky Mix (10), questions 2 and 4 of a stop round are due items with a visible tag and move to box 1 due tomorrow, Tricky Mix plays 7 rebuilt items and books 7 fixed).

### P1-08 Mastery tracking and in-round adaptation (v53, 2026-11-10, Claude)
- What changed: new `src/js/51-mastery.js` (ROADMAP 3.3). Every first try at a question of a map stop (any round) updates `S.mast[nid] = {n, p, ms, d, last, md}`: attempts, first-try accuracy (plain average for 10 tries, then moving average 0.1), running median answer time (ms, capped 60 s), distinct days, last day and mastered day as day numbers (`STRK.idx`). `masteryState(nid)` = new / learning / practised (>= 70% over 10+) / mastered (>= 90% over 20+ on 2+ days). `harder(nd)` / `easier(nd)` = next / previous stop of the same skill in path order. In a normal stop round, after question 5: 5 of 5 right with a median under 12 s (16 s Pre-K, K) makes the rest Challenge questions from `harder()` (bolt "Challenge!" tag, pet line; a wrong first try is forgiven for the star rating); 2 or fewer right turns on support (hint shown before every first try, questions 6, 8, 10 from `easier()`); 3 wrong first tries in a row (any time) give an easier question with the hint shown and the pet saying "Here's a hint!", and after question 5 step the mode down (challenge -> normal, normal -> support). Middling rounds are unchanged. Star thresholds, round length, coins, locks unchanged.
- Hooks in `50-round.js` (4 lines): `makeQ` first asks `mastQ()`; `check` calls `mastTry(q, ok)` on a first try; `renderQ` calls `mastShow(q)` before `fit()`; `finish` rates stars on `min(n, score + G.forgive)`.
- Saved data: new optional field `S.mast`, read with defaults, created on the first answer; no migration (LATEST stays 5). Old code ignores the field.
- Tests: test.js check 23 "mastery" (no S.mast on every fixture reads as new and changes nothing; state thresholds incl. 2 days; median ignores an outlier and follows a shift; demo skills and `nomast` rounds are not recorded; a 100%-fast bot gets challenge from question 6 on every stop at 4 grades; slow 100% bot does not; forgiven challenge misses; 300 rounds of a 30% bot get support, hints first, easier stops on 6/8/10, and an easier question after every 3-wrong run; a middling bot on fresh, midway, maxed gets exactly the old questions (own stop or the usual review), no tags, no hints; determinism; boss rounds record but never adapt; 400 stops = 28,991 bytes). smoke.js P1-08 step (midway: Even or Odd as a new stop, Challenge questions 6-10 with a visible tag, every other one missed once, still 3 stars, record saved; a support round shows the hint before the first try). shots.js `challenge`, `support`.
- Files: src/js/51-mastery.js (new), src/js/50-round.js (hooks), src/css/33-mastery.css (new, tokens only), tools/test.js, smoke.js, shots.js, contrast.js (1 pair), check.sh (hex guard covers 33-mastery.css), build.py (VER), built output.
- Checklist 7.1: only chunk files changed (58-playtime.js not needed, see Ideas); VER bumped; build, test.js (23 checks), smoke.js and sizes via check.sh; screenshots challenge 600x960 and 1024x600, support 380x780 looked at; midway fixture keeps stars, coins, pet and map progress (test); no new emoji, no raw hex.

### P1-07 Placement quiz "Find my level" (v52, 2026-11-10, Claude)
- What changed: new `src/js/45-placement.js`. `PLACE.*` is a pure state machine (world list, seed, right answers per testlet in; next testlet or result out; no S, no DOM): starts at the first world with g >= grade - 1, 4-question testlets of stops from one world, 3-4 right up, 0-1 down, 2 right one more testlet (6 of 8 passes), stops when a "not yet" world sits right above a passed one, at either end, or after 5 testlets (20 questions). Result: the world above the highest passed world (the lowest world asked when none passed); the worlds below are skipped. The screen reuses the question kinds (KIND render/bind) with one try, no stars, no buzz, no hints, a neutral "Got it!" in brand blue and 4 progress dots; the X quits at any time and nothing is saved before the end. Result screen: the start world, the skipped worlds ("you can still play them"), Go to my map (opens on that world), the other subject, Home.
- Offered on Home to a new child (K and up, no math or reading stop passed, never placed: a card with Math, Reading, Not now) and in Grown-ups ("Find my level" row under Grade, with the last result). Not for Pre-K, not for Spanish.
- Saved data (no migration; read with defaults): `S.place[path] = {world, date, asked, right, g}` (world id, today(), grade at the time) and `S.skip[worldId] = 1` for the worlds below the result. placeApply only adds: no star, stop, coin or earlier skip is removed, nothing re-locks; a later run replaces `S.place[path]` only.
- Edits to existing files: `42-paths.js` startWorld: one line `const pw=placeWorld(p);if(pw>=0)return pw;` (the placed world while the grade is unchanged); curNode: a world below the start world that placement skipped is passed over even if it has some progress (`||worldSkipped(p,wi)`). `46-home.js` home: `${placeBanner()}` before "Pick a game!" and `placeBannerBind();` after the game buttons. `80-grown.js`: `${placeGrownHTML()}` after the grade picker and `placeGrownBind();` after gradePickBind. New `src/css/39-place.css` (tokens only).
- Tests: test.js check 23 "placement" (start world per grade, scripted rules incl. 2-right repeat and 6 of 8, step is pure, all 10,553 answer sequences on 3 path shapes end within 20 questions with a valid result, items are real stops of the world and reproducible and valid, 2,000 simulated students 97.9% within one world, fixtures fresh/midway/maxed x all right/all wrong/mixed: only place and skip change, no stop re-locks, start world = result, other grade ignores it, a lower re-run keeps skips, Pre-K not offered, no raw hex in the CSS). smoke.js step "P1-07" at 380x780: card on Home (48 px), quitting after 2 answers leaves S and the stored save unchanged, 16 right answers by real taps place at Space Station with all 4 math worlds skipped, stars and coins unchanged, map opens on Space Station, Grown-ups shows the result. shots.js group place-home, place-intro, place-q, place-done, place-grown.
- Checklist 7.1: chunk files plus the two small 42-paths.js edits (needed so the map starts at the result); VER bumped (v52, provisional); build, test.js (23 checks), smoke.js and sizes via check.sh; screenshots looked at: place-home, place-q, place-done, place-intro at 380x780, place-grown at 600x960, place-q at 1024x600 (quit and Hear it buttons made 48 px after the smoke size check caught 42 px); midway keeps its stars, coins, pet and map progress (test); no new emoji, no raw hex.

### P5-05 Activity log, time on task (v51, 2026-11-10, Claude)
- What changed: new `src/js/53-activity.js` (`ACT`). `S.log = [{d, sec, rounds, q, ok, by:{area:sec}}]`, oldest first, 60 days. A question's time runs from when it appears to when it is finally answered right, capped at 60 s, and leaves out time while the tab is hidden (own visibilitychange listener); a question never answered adds nothing. Re-asked misses add time but not a question. A finished round adds 1 round; Fact Sprint adds its active seconds to math. Subject = the skill's `area` (math, read, es; later bt, disc).
- API for P5-06 (documented in the file header): `ACT.get()`, `ACT.entry(key)`, `ACT.sum(endKey, days)` -> `{sec, rounds, q, ok, by, active}` (this week = `ACT.sum(today(),7)`, last week = `ACT.sum(ACT.shift(today(),-7),7)`; accuracy = ok/q), `ACT.shift(key,n)`, `ACT.total()`. All safe on a missing or damaged `S.log`.
- Hooks (one line each): 50-round.js `renderQ` start `actQStart(q.sk);`, after `K.right(q);` `actQEnd(G.tries==0&&!G.retry,G.retry);`, in `finish()` after the prizes line `actRound();`; 57-fluency.js `finish` after `FLU.record(...)` `actFlu(sec);`. Hooks never throw into the game.
- Saved data: additive only, no migration (read with defaults, rule 6). 60 heavy days (3 subjects, 150 questions, about 70 min a day) stays under 6 KB (tested).
- Tests: test.js check 23 (idle cap, hidden time, start-hidden, re-asked miss, rounds, Fact Sprint, day boundary, 60-day trim, future days, size, missing/damaged/duplicate data, hooks). smoke.js: new step `// P5-05` plays a real round and checks the saved entry.
- Files: src/js/53-activity.js, 50-round.js, 57-fluency.js, tools/test.js, smoke.js, build.py (VER), built output. No UI, so no screenshots.
- Checklist 7.1: only chunk files changed; VER bumped (provisional v51, renumbered at merge); build, test.js, smoke.js (network blocked) and sizes via check.sh pass; no new emoji or colours; no saved field of the existing save changes (midway fixture unaffected, `S.log` only added).

### P1-03 New kinds A: number pads, multi-select, order (v47, 2026-11-10, Claude)
- What changed: `KIND.num` gets `q.pad` ('int' default, 'dec' adds `.`, 'neg' adds `±`, 'frac' two stacked slots, answer `'3/4'`), `q.sep` ('R': quotient and remainder slots, `'9R2'`; 'mix': whole + fraction in three slots, `'2 1/3'`), `q.eq:'value'` (numeric compare: `0.50` = `.5`, `2/4` = `1/2`, exact BigInt maths; the default stays exact text) and `q.max` (digits per slot, default 4, up to 9). With more than one slot the check key shows `→` and moves to the next slot, `✓` on the last slot submits, del steps back across the separator, the active slot is outlined. New kinds `multi` (choose all that apply, a Check button, `ans` = sorted array) and `order` (tap tiles into slots, tap a filled slot to take it back, auto-checks when full; generalises `build`, which is unchanged). Each has render/bind/right/retry/reveal/spoken/validate plus `same`. Existing int pad behaviour is unchanged (same HTML, same keys).
- Engine hook (50-round.js `check`, 2 small edits): `ok=K.same?K.same(q,v):String(v)===String(q.ans)` (optional per-kind answer comparison) and the star/burst source falls back to `$('.inp')` when a kind has no slot or button to point at. Speed Race is untouched (it only plays int `num`).

### P1-05 Stable world IDs, grade tags, world gating, long maps (v46, 2026-11-10, Claude)
- What changed: every world has a permanent `id` and grade tag `g` (CURRICULUM 2: `m_meadow` K, `m_candy` 1, `m_reef` 1, `m_space` 2, `r_meadow` K, `r_candy` 1, `r_reef` 1, `r_space` 2, `e_plaza`/`e_fiesta`/`e_repaso` -1 with `byLevel`). Nodes carry `wid`; a boss is keyed `boss_<worldId>`; `S.wdone`, `S.skip` and the game-time repeat keys use world ids. Gating per ROADMAP 3.2 in 42-paths.js: a world is open if it is first, `g <= grade` (not on `byLevel` paths), skipped, the previous world is finished (boss beaten, all passed, or skipped), or it has progress; passed stops are always open; a stop behind the child's progress is never locked; unpassed stops in a world whose boss is beaten show a NEW tag. Test out also writes `S.skip`.
- Long maps (46-home.js): a world strip above the map (one chip per world, grouped by grade label, check or lock icon, design tokens and `ico()`), the map draws only the picked world and its neighbours, "more worlds" buttons at the ends, opens on the child's current world (or the one just played). `ptCalc` "harder world" uses `worldStep()` (grade-relative; equals the old index rule when no grade is set).
- API for P1-06 (42-paths.js header): `GRADES`, `gradeLabel(g)`, `gradeOf()` (S.grade, else 0 = K), `setGrade(g)` (validates, saves), `worldOpen`, `worldFinished`, `unlocked`, `stopNew`, `startWorld`, `curNode`, `curWorld`, `worldStep`, `worldHasProgress`, `worldId`, `buildNodes(p)` (tests).
- Saved data: migration 3 (LATEST 4): boss stars `boss<k>` copied to `boss_<worldId>`, `S.wdone` `math0` keys copied to world ids, fully passed worlds marked done, `S.ptRep` index keys (shared by all paths) moved to every path's world key. Old keys kept (rule 9). Index k always means world k of the frozen `MAP_V42` table; migrations 1 and 2 now use the same table (identical output today).

### P1-04 New kinds B: match, tap, spell (v44, 2026-11-10, Claude)
- What changed: three new entries at the end of `KIND` in `src/js/49-kinds.js` (marked `// ---- P1-04 kinds ----`), each with render/bind/right/retry/reveal/spoken/validate. `match`: `items` and shuffled `opts` ([{v,l,h}], 3 to 5 pairs), tap one then its partner, matched pairs get their own colour; a wrong pair submits `'!'` (first one = miss and retry, second = reveal all pairs), all pairs right submits `q.ans` (sorted values joined by `|`). `tap`: `q.html` holds elements with `data-v`; tapping submits the value (they get role=button, tabindex, Enter/Space). `spell`: `word`, optional `lang:'es'` (adds the n/accent row), `.lslots` slots in html plus a visible clue, in-app QWERTY keyboard with a backspace icon key; wrong word keeps the right start (like build). ### FIX: number pad reveal shows the wrong number (v42, 2026-11-10, Claude)
- Cause: in `KIND.num.bind` (49-kinds.js) the slot was written with the typed value after `submit()`. On the second wrong try `submit()` reveals the answer, then that write put the child's wrong number back (orange "answer" style). Same in Speed Race. Pre-existing since v30; kept as-is through P1-02 on purpose.
- Fix: after a "go" tap that locked the question, leave the slot to the engine (one line).
- Test: smoke step 7 plays a math round to the first number question, enters a wrong number twice, and checks the slot shows the right answer. Proved it fails without the fix ("shows 9, expected 3").
- Tests: check.sh ok.

### P5-04 Streak calendar (v39, 2026-11-10, Claude)
- What changed: new `src/js/55-streak.js` (`STRK` object: date helpers, `calc` pure streak count, `tick`, month `grid`; `streakTick()`, `streakCal(off)` screen) and `src/css/35-streak.css` (tokens only). `finish()` in 50-round.js calls `streakTick()` once per finished learning round (one line) and shows the reward line in the finish messages (one line). Home gets a flame chip (`#stkb`) beside the coins chip; tapping opens the month grid (stars on active days, moon on rest days, today outlined, back up to 4 months). Rest rule: one missed day is forgiven if the day before it was played and no other rest day is in the 7 days before it. Rewards at 3, 7, 14, 30 days: +5, +10, +20, +50 coins and a sticker, once per run (`rw` resets when the streak breaks).
- Saved: `S.streak = {days: {'Y-M-D': rounds}, best, rw}`; days trimmed to 120; read with defaults when missing or damaged (`STRK.get()`), no migration needed (additive; LATEST stays 3). Keys use the same local-time format as `today()`; day arithmetic is UTC on the calendar date so daylight saving cannot shift a day. Future-dated keys (clock set back) are ignored, never counted.

### P5-01: Fact Sprint (v36), 2026-11-10, Claude
- What changed: home button "Fact Sprint" (6th action tile). Sets by progress: add10 always, sub10 once `sub` level 1 is passed, add20 once `add` level 2, sub20 once `sub` level 2. Two modes per set: 60 seconds (most right) and 20 facts (fastest time). Big keypad, auto-submit when the digit count matches, no hints. A wrong answer shows the fact for 1 s and it returns after 3 other facts; the same fact never appears twice in a row. End screen: score, best, 7-day sparkline, "NEW RECORD!" with confetti. Game time: 1 min for the first sprint of each set per day (60 s mode needs 5 right), +1 min for any new record; extra sprints are free practice. The clock does not count time while the tab is hidden. Timers are stopped by `view()` via `stopFlu()`.
- Saved: `S.flu = {key: {best, hist: [[day, sec, right]] (30)}}`; key is the set id (60 s) or set id + `-20`; `S.flu[set].paid` is the day game time was last earned. No migration: absent field reads as empty (additive, section 9 rule 6). `S.v` unchanged.
- Files: new `src/js/57-fluency.js`; `46-home.js` (button), `40-view.js` (calls `stopFlu`), `58-playtime.js` (one line in the Game Room earn list), `tools/test.js` (block at the end, `// ---- P5-01 fact sprint ----`), `tools/smoke.js` (plays a 20-facts sprint), `tools/shots.js` (`fluency`, `fluency-modes`, `sprint`, `sprint-end`), `tools/build.py` (v36).

### P0-01: Test and screenshot harness (v31), 2026-11-10, Claude
- New `tools/package.json` (devDependency `playwright-core` only, uses system Chrome via `CHROME` or `/usr/bin/google-chrome`, no browser download) and `.gitignore` (`tools/node_modules`, `tools/out`).
- `tools/lib/browser.js`: launches Chrome headless, opens built `index.html` over `file://`, seeds `localStorage['starschool-v2']` from a fixture, blocks and records all http(s) requests, collects console errors and uncaught exceptions.
- `tools/shots.js [--fixture=name] <screen...>`: PNGs to `tools/out/` at 600x960, 1024x600, 380x780. Screens: setup, home, map, map-read, map-es, question, question-read, boss, finish, gameroom, grown, shop, stickers.
- `tools/smoke.js`: bot does fresh setup, one full 10/10 round on each path through the real UI (reads `G.q.ans`), Game Room, Grown-ups (long press on the gear), loads the `midway` fixture and checks stars, coins, pet hat and map progress; fails on any console error, exception or network request.
- `tools/fixtures/fresh.json`, `midway.json` (v30 save: progress in all three paths, misses, coins, wearables), `maxed.json`.
- `tools/check.sh`: build, `test.js`, `smoke.js`, size report (fails over 1.5 MB, warns over 1.2 MB).
- No app code changed. `VER` v30 -> v31.
- Results: `bash tools/check.sh` exit 0; test.js 207000 generated, 0 errors; smoke OK, zero console errors, zero network requests; index.html 374,697 bytes. Looked at home 600x960, question 380x780, finish 1024x600.
